Not done: DPI is covered by DIP column widths and a 900 × 500 DIP window, not a 200 % render; the matrix keeps the panel's fixed 560-DIP height rather than filling the workspace; a cell's full detail (listing id, last error) is only in the Liste view — the matrix cell shows marker + word (a tooltip/legend is the next issue, #843); the matrix is rebuilt on every filter keystroke (a 1000 × 20 pivot is cheap, but there is no debounce).

## #843 — Channel matrix status legend (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-843-channel-matrix-legend off codex/issue-842-channel-matrix-sticky-axes (f056855)

New issue. DESIGN: the matrix cells had markers (#842) but nothing explained them, the marker table lived inside the cell record, and the one real "unsupported" fact — a channel this build can only plan for locally — was not a state at all.

`ChannelMatrixLegend.cs` (new): the one table of matrix states — SYNCED ✔ yayında, PENDING ⏳ bekliyor, DRAFT ✎ taslak, STALE ◔ bayat, MISSING ○ eşleme yok, ERROR ✖ hata, AUTH_ERROR ⚠ bağlantı, LOCAL_ONLY ⊘ yalnız yerel, NONE · kayıt yok — each with a glyph, a word, a description and a level. `ServiceStatuses` names the six mapping statuses the listing service records, and the tests prove each has its entry. `For(mapping, auth, localOnly)`: a channel without live capability first (its connection status is meaningless), then a dead connection, then the recorded mapping state; an unknown state falls back to "kayıt yok" and is never invented into something else; the two modifier keys are not reachable as mapping statuses. `Counts(matrix)` lists only the states present, in legend order, with counts. `ChannelMatrixCell` now delegates marker, word, label and level to the legend (a cell and the legend cannot disagree) and carries `LocalOnly`, which the pivot takes from the service's own capabilities text (`ChannelListingMatrixService.LocalOnlyCapabilities` / `IsLocalOnly`) — the connection catalogue's fact, not a guess. `ChannelMatrixRow.Descriptions` exposes a description per cell.

Wired: `ChannelListingMatrixPanel.cs` — a legend ("channel-matrix-legend") between the filter bar and the matrix, rebuilt on every refresh: one chip per state on screen ("○ eşleme yok (2)"), each a focusable tab stop with `ToolTipService.ShowsToolTipOnKeyboardFocus`, the description as tooltip and `AutomationProperties.HelpText`, accent and border weight from `SeverityStyle` (system colours under high contrast; the glyph + word carry the meaning regardless); "Gösterilecek hücre yok." when nothing is on screen. Every store cell of the matrix carries its legend description as a tooltip that also opens from keyboard focus. The #842 fixtures used "Yerel plan" as filler capability text — now meaningful — and were moved to a live capability; the empty cell's word became "kayıt yok".

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixLegendTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first run caught that saved-but-untested connections read AUTH_ERROR and that local-only must outrank it): every recorded status has an entry, glyphs and words distinct, descriptions present; precedence local-only > connection > mapping, case-insensitive, unknown → "kayıt yok", modifier keys unreachable as statuses; a cell agrees with the legend; counts list only present states in order (Etsy synced/error, Trendyol local-only whatever its mapping, eBay's cell a connection problem, empties counted), `IsLocalOnly`, a description per cell. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixLegendUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel with an Etsy store (never tested → AUTH_ERROR) and a Trendyol store (local-only in this build) — the legend shows exactly "⚠ bağlantı (2)" and "⊘ yalnız yerel (2)", chips are tab stops with keyboard tooltips and help text, every chip leads with a glyph, the Trendyol cells read "⊘ yalnız yerel" with the capability description, and a store cell carries the description as a keyboard-openable tooltip.

## #844 — Channel matrix readiness filters (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-844-channel-matrix-readiness-filters off codex/issue-843-channel-matrix-legend (7ba9826)

New issue. DESIGN: the matrix could be narrowed by text, channel, shop and one raw status code, but not by the states it actually shows, and never by several at once; nothing said how many products a filter hid, nor that a filter had emptied the screen.

`ChannelMatrixReadinessFilter.cs` (new): `Apply(matrix, activeStates)` → `ChannelMatrixReadinessResult(Matrix, Counts, Active, HiddenRows)` — the legend's states as "any of" row filters (a row stays when any of its cells is in an active state; no active state means every row), columns never dropped, counts always from the unfiltered matrix (a chip says what exists even while another chip hides it), only legend keys honoured (an unknown key is dropped, never invented into a state or an accidentally empty screen), `IsEmpty` / `EmptyText` naming the filters that hid everything; `Toggle` returns a new set. One walk over the cells: 100 000 cells filter in milliseconds (pinned by test).

Wired: `ChannelListingMatrixPanel.cs` — the legend chips (#843) are now `ToggleButton`s that toggle a state in the filter set (still focusable tab stops with keyboard tooltips; the tooltip says whether the filter is on; `AutomationProperties.Name` "hata: 3 hücre, filtre açık"), a "Filtreyi kaldır" button appears while any filter is active, `RefreshGrid` applies the filter to the pivot before rendering and shows "channel-matrix-empty" with the zero-result text when nothing is left; the status line adds "N ürün durum filtresiyle gizli". Counts are recomputed on every text/channel/shop change (a store switch), from that view's whole matrix.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixReadinessFilterTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first run caught my own miscount of a fixture): no filter → all rows; one state → its rows with the hidden count and unchanged whole-matrix counts and columns; three states (mixed case) combine as any-of; a state with no cells → empty with the state named and "filtreyi kaldırın"; unknown keys dropped; the empty-matrix text; toggle is pure. 100 000 cells: every cell counted once, filtered under 1.5 s (measured in milliseconds). `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixReadinessFilterUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel (Etsy S1 never tested → connection problem, Trendyol T1 local-only) — two off chips that are focusable tab stops; toggling "bağlantı" keeps both rows, reads "filtre açık" and shows the reset; switching the store filter to T1 empties the matrix with the named zero-result text while the local-only chip still counts that store's whole view; adding "yalnız yerel" brings the rows back (any-of); clearing the store filter shows both chips on; the reset clears both. The #843 UI test now finds the chips as toggle buttons.

## #845 — Channel matrix bulk preview drawer (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-845-channel-matrix-bulk-drawer off codex/issue-844-channel-matrix-readiness-filters (dc80ae5)

`ChannelMatrixBulk.cs` (new): the drawer's model. `Revision(rows)` — an order-independent SHA-256 fingerprint (16 hex) of product × store × mapping state × listing × auth per flat row, the "matrix as shown"; `Compose(preview, target, allowedStoreKeys, revisionAtPreview, selectedCount)` — lines re-derived from the bulk preview (READY when Before ≠ After, SKIP "plan zaten aynı" when identical, ERROR with a sanitized reason), affected / skipped / errors counts, the first 50 lines plus `LinesTruncated`, and a `CanApply` with one plain reason: empty selection, target not among the offered stores ("yanlış mağazaya yazılmaz"), no preview, or nothing to change; `CheckBeforeApply(revisionAtPreview, revisionNow, allowedStoreKeys, targetKey, appliedPreviews, previewId)` — the guards that run again at the click: already applied (idempotency), store no longer offered (wrong store), matrix changed since the preview (stale revision).

`ChannelMatrixBulkDrawer.cs` (new): the drawer itself on `DialogShell` (destructive layout: Enter is Vazgeç). A target store combo listing only the pivot's columns (the stores the shell offers), defaulting to the one store the selected cells share; a target category; "Önizle" running `BulkProductOperations.Preview` (`SetChannelMapping` → local `ChannelProductPlan`s only) off the UI thread under a cancellation token — products deleted since the matrix loaded become ERROR lines instead of vanishing; the summary "Hedef: … · N seçili ürün · A uygulanacak · S zaten aynı · E engelli", the reason line, the line list with "… ve N daha", an approval checkbox enabled only when something can be applied, a status line. "Onayla ve uygula" is refused while a preview or apply runs, without a preview, without the box, or when `CheckBeforeApply` says no (the refusal goes to the status line and the box is cleared); otherwise `Apply(preview, approved: true, token, progress)` runs off the UI thread, the preview id is recorded in the owner's ledger, the result is handed back and the window closes. Vazgeç cancels the token (a running preview stops; an apply stops in its validation phase; the commit phase finishes, as `BulkProductOperations` documents) and closes; the X is held only while a write is in flight. No live marketplace write exists on this path.

Wired: `ChannelListingMatrixPanel.cs` — the matrix's cell selection is now Extended (Shift/Ctrl, a whole store column); the "Toplu plan…" command ("channel-matrix-bulk") takes the selected cells' distinct products, the store they share as the default target, the revision of the loaded rows, a fresh-revision function (rebuilt from the stores at the click), the shell's `allowedStoreKeys`, the panel-level applied-preview ledger, and shows the drawer modally; after an apply the status line keeps "Son toplu plan: N yazıldı · S atlandı · E engelli" across the refresh that follows. An empty selection or the Liste view is refused on the error surface before any drawer opens.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixBulkTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): counts, markers and the sanitized error reason; refusals for empty selection, wrong store, no change and no preview; the 1000-line cap (50 shown, 950 more); the revision ignores order and moves on a state/listing change or a lost row; apply refused when stale, wrong-store or repeated, allowed for a fresh preview. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixBulkUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel with 60 products × 2 stores — an empty selection is refused on the error surface with no owned window; the whole Etsy column selected → the modal drawer (driven from inside ShowDialog's nested loop) defaults to Etsy · S1, previews "60 uygulanacak", lists 50 with "… ve 10 daha", refuses apply without the box, then writes 60 local plans (category, no listing id) to Etsy only and the panel refreshes with the result note; the same selection again previews "60 zaten aynı", refuses, and Cancel closes without writing; on a drawer built for three products: a store removed from the offered set after the preview, a changed revision, and an already applied preview id each refuse at the click with nothing written; Cancel closes with nothing written; a clean apply writes three, closes, reports and records the preview. The UI test caught one defect before commit: the drawer's own Closing guard held the window open after a successful apply.

## #846 — Report catalog card layout (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-846-report-catalog-cards off codex/issue-845-channel-matrix-bulk-drawer (e71fb74)

`ReportCatalog.cs` (new): the catalog is a fixed table of the reports this build really produces — nine definitions (product Excel and XML exports, rejected import rows, product order summary, orders list, listing matrix, data quality, API health, support package), each with its purpose, the local data it reads, Global/Store scope, output kinds and the owner route. `Describe` turns one into a card: title trimmed at 60 characters for the card and kept whole for the tooltip and the accessible name; "Kapsam: tüm veri · …" or "Kapsam: N sunulan mağaza · …"; the last run as "Son çalıştırma: 3 dk önce · başarılı · 1.234 satır" (failed/cancelled without a row count) or "Hiç çalıştırılmadı"; the saved-filter count; "Çıktı: Excel, CSV"; hidden when store-scoped and the shell offers no store. `Build` gives cards + hidden + total with a search over title/purpose/sources and one empty text per cause (no reports / store-scoped hidden / search); `Load` reads the real run store and the real saved views (`UiPreferenceStore` module "report:<key>"). `ReportRunStore` (new, `report_runs.db`): `Record(key, started, state, rows, note?, store?)` validates the key (lowercase letters/digits/dash), refuses `Never`, clamps rows, keeps only a file name from a note and sanitizes it; `Latest()` is the newest run per key; `Recent(key, n)`.

`ReportsPanel.cs` (new): the "Raporlar" workspace — heading, search ("report-search"), "Yenile", the count line ("report-count": "9 rapor · 4 mağaza kapsamlı rapor gizli (sunulan mağaza yok) · N aramaya uymadı"), the empty text ("report-empty") and the card grid ("report-cards"): one 300-DIP focusable `Button` per card (title / purpose / scope / last run with the severity glyph and accent / saved filters / outputs; tooltip and `AutomationProperties.Name` carry the whole title and the run; the tooltip opens from the keyboard); Enter or click opens the owner route. `Render(host, view, open)` is the public layout step.

Wired: `MainWindow.Navigation.cs` registers the `reports` route (YÖNETİM, before diagnostics) with the shell's `AllowedStoreKeys` — the screen-parity audit that logged "Ekran paritesi BLOCKED: reports" at every start is now complete, the dashboard's "Raporlar" quick card (gated by `routeExists` since #813) appears, and the help topic "reports" describes the page that now exists. Real runs feed the cards: the product Excel export (`MainWindow.Excel.cs`), the rejected-rows export (Succeeded / Cancelled / Failed with the written count, including the cancellation path), the product XML export (`MainWindow.xaml.cs`) and the support package (`DiagnosticsPanel.cs`) each record a run.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportCatalogTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): card texts for never / succeeded / failed / cancelled runs, scope by offered stores, filters and outputs, an 80-character title trimmed to 60 with the whole title in the accessible name; zero definitions; fifty (25 store-scoped hidden without a store, the empty text naming the cause); search including a zero result; runs and filters for keys outside the catalog ignored; catalog keys unique and every title within the limit; the run store keeps the latest per key, keeps a file name and drops a token/password from a note, refuses bad keys and `Never`. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportsPanelUiTests.cs` (new, 2): the real panel — nine focusable, named, 300-DIP cards all "Hiç çalıştırılmadı"; a card takes keyboard focus; a recorded run and a saved view change the cards on Yenile; with no offered store the four store-scoped cards go and the count says so; search narrows to the support package, names a zero result and restores; a card navigates to its owner (diagnostics); fifty rendered cards wrap into rows at 300 DIP with the trimmed title on the card and the whole one in the tooltip. The real shell: `routes` contains "reports", `ScreenParityAudit` is complete, Navigate reaches it, and the cards follow the shell's own store list.

## #847 — Report parameter panel (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-847-report-parameter-panel off codex/issue-846-report-catalog-cards (15d25fa)

`ReportParameters.cs` (new): the typed parameter set (`ReportParameterSet`: store key, whole-day UTC from/to, delivery state, query) and the schema per report (`SchemaFor`: store for store-scoped reports, date range for the order reports, delivery state for the order lists, query for every list; the support package and API health take nothing); `Defaults` = the first offered store, the last 30 days, every state; `Validate` names the field: no store / a store the shell does not offer ("yanlış mağaza için rapor üretilmez") / an inverted range / a range over 366 days / missing dates / an unknown state / an over-long or control-character query are blocking, a future end date and a stray store on a global report only warn; `Serialize`/`Deserialize` round-trip those fields and nothing else (ISO dates; garbage, oversized or wrong-shaped payloads load as null; hostile values as empty) — a saved filter can carry neither a credential nor a customer field; `Summary` is the one-line note for runs and statuses.

`ReportRunner.cs` (new): the one report the workspace runs itself — the orders list as CSV ("orders-csv", added to the catalog as a store-scoped CSV report owned by the reports route). `RunAsync` validates again (the panel's check is not enough), refuses a store not offered, a foreign report and a non-CSV path, reads the store's orders page by page (`OrdersStore.ReadPage` by shop, marketplace matched case-insensitively), keeps the whole-day range by the record's update time and the delivery state ("Unknown" also means "no package"), renders through `ReportTemplateRenderer` — whose allow-list has no customer field — writes the file whole and moves it into place (no temp file survives), and records Succeeded / Cancelled / Failed in `ReportRunStore` with the parameter summary as the note.

`ReportParameterPanel.cs` (new): the run setup — `FormField` rows (label, required marker spoken as "zorunlu", help, validation slot) for the store combo (only the offered stores; disabled with the reason when none), two date pickers, the delivery-state combo (Tümü + the order states), the query box; a validation summary line; saved filters (combo + name + Yükle / Kaydet / Sil under `UiPreferenceStore` module "report:<key>"; an unreadable payload says so and keeps the current values); "Çalıştır" (Enter) for the runnable report, "Ekranda aç" for the others, a status line; every control a labelled tab stop. `ReportsPanel.cs`: the grid now has the setup beside it ("report-setup-host"); a card selects its report instead of navigating; the run asks for the file with the standard save dialog (or the injected `choosePath`) and refreshes the cards after.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportParametersTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): schemas per report, defaults with and without stores, every validation rule with its field, the future-date warning, the round-trip codec with garbage / oversized / hostile payloads, the summary line (culture-formatted dates); the runner on five real orders in two stores — the store, range, state ("Unknown" = no package) and query filters, the header and rows of the CSV, the empty result, five recorded runs with the note, the guards that record nothing, a cancelled token that writes nothing, a write that cannot land recorded as Failed with no temp file left, and the renderer refusing a customer column. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportParameterPanelUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel — the orders CSV card opens its setup with the defaults; an inverted range blocks in the field and the summary and re-enables when fixed; no store blocks; a filter saves, loads back, and a hostile one loads as unreadable; every control is a tab stop (a DatePicker's inner text box carries it); the run writes the CSV the panel was told to, the card shows "başarılı · 1 satır" and the setup stays; a screen report offers "Ekranda aç" and navigates; the support package says it has no parameters; without an offered store the store field is off with the reason and the run disabled. The #846 UI test now selects a card and navigates from the setup.

## #848 — Report run progress surface (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-848-report-run-progress off codex/issue-847-report-parameter-panel (c173d38)

`ReportRunProgress.cs` (new): the run as three stages — Sorgu, Üretim, Dışa aktarma — fed only by the runner's real events (`ReportRunProgressEvent`: stage, status, done, optional total, note). `ReportRunProgressState` mirrors the import stepper's rules: a stage with an unknown total shows a count and an indeterminate bar, never a guessed percentage; a known total gives a percentage; starting a stage resets everything after it; `Cancel` lands on the running stage (the query when nothing runs yet) and leaves the rest pending; a failure keeps its note — passed through the uncapped redaction — as `Diagnostics`; `Headline` is the one line ("Sorgu sürüyor · 1.200 / 4.000 (%30)", "Dışa aktarma başarısız: …", "Üretim iptal", "Tamamlandı"); glyph and word per status.

`ReportRunner.cs`: `RunAsync` now takes `IProgress<ReportRunProgressEvent>` and emits the stages as they happen — query Running with no total, then page by page with the store's real total, Done; generate Running/Done over the row count; export Running/Done — and on cancellation or failure marks the stage it was in (a failed export carries the sanitized message, never the path). The run note and the new audit event (module "reports", action "run:orders-csv", outcome, store) carry the parameter summary without the query text: `ReportParameters.Summary(…, includeQuery: false)` writes "Arama: var" — the operator's own search text is never logged.

`ReportParameterPanel.cs`: the run view under the actions — a headline, one row per stage (glyph + label, counter + status word + elapsed, a ProgressBar that is indeterminate while the total is unknown, with an accessible name), an "İptal" button while a stage runs (cancels the run's token; Escape triggers it), terminal diagnostics after a failure or cancellation (blocking colour for a failure, warning for a cancellation), "Tanılamaya git" only for a failure, and "Yeniden dene" that re-runs the same parameters. A run that ends without the runner closing its stage is marked cancelled, never left spinning. `ReportsPanel.cs`: a retry re-runs into the file the operator already chose for that report — no second save dialog.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportRunProgressTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): the stage model (unknown total → indeterminate and no percentage, known total → 25 %, elapsed, cancel on the running stage with earlier stages kept and later ones pending, failure diagnostics with the token redacted, restart resetting later stages, complete headline); the runner on three real orders — the first query event knows no total, the total appears page by page, the stage sequence ends with export Done and replays to a complete state, the run note and the audit detail carry no query text ("musteri@example.com" never lands), a cancelled token marks the query stage, a blocked export marks the export stage with a note free of the path. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportRunProgressUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel — a run into a directory shows ✔ Sorgu / ✖ Dışa aktarma, sanitized diagnostics, "Tanılamaya git" (navigates) and "Yeniden dene"; the retry asks for no file again; a good run shows every row ✔, "Tamamlandı", full bars, no retry, and the card's run text; on the setup with a slow run the query bar is indeterminate, the second run is refused, İptal cancels the token, the query row reads ⏹ with the cancellation sentence, retry appears without a diagnostics button, and the retry finishes.

The assertion now uses fragments a timestamp cannot contain ("+90 532", "532 123"). Test-only change: 572 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release); the artifact is the #848 one (SHA-256 655e07d5…).

## #849 — Report result column chooser (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-849-report-result-columns off codex/issue-848-report-run-progress (ece0bbb)

`ReportColumns.cs` (new): the orders report's column schema — seven columns with labels and groups a person recognises (Kimlik / Durum / Finans / Zaman / Kargo), one classified: "Kargo takip (maskeli)". `Default` shows every column at its DIP width except the classified one; `Resolve(schema, persistedJson, classifiedAllowed)` applies a persisted layout through the shared `DataGridLayoutCodec` (keys, DIP widths, `ResolveOrder`) — a saved column the schema lost is dropped, a column the schema gained appears at its default position, a width outside 40–600 DIP falls back, a classified column stays hidden while the policy is off whatever was saved, and at least one column always shows; `Persist`, `Toggle` (the last visible column cannot be hidden; a classified one cannot be shown against the policy), `Move`, `Reorder` (the grid's order for the visible columns, hidden ones kept in place), `Resize`, `Grouped(layout, query)` (groups in order of first appearance, search by label/key/group) and `Summary` ("5 / 7 kolon görünür · 1 sınıflandırılmış kolon politika kapalı").

`ReportRunner.cs`: the run is now two calls — `QueryAsync` (stages query and generate) returns a `ReportResult` for the grid; `ExportAsync(result, columns, path)` (stage export) writes exactly the chosen columns, refusing an empty set, a column outside the schema and a non-CSV path. The rows carry the tracking column already masked through `OrderShippingSection.MaskTracking`, so neither the grid nor a file can show a raw number; `ReportTemplateRenderer` allows "Tracking" for that masked value and exposes `IsAllowed`. `ReportColumnChooserDialog.cs` (new): the chooser on `DialogShell` — search, grouped checkboxes (a classified column listed but disabled with "politika kapalı" while the policy is off), the selected row moved up/down from the keyboard, "Varsayılan", Kaydet handing the layout back.

`ReportParameterPanel.cs`: "Çalıştır" now lists the result on screen — a summary ("2 satır · 6 / 7 kolon görünür"), "Kolonlar…", "CSV'ye aktar", and a DataGrid whose columns come from the resolved layout (label header, `[Key]` binding, DIP width, the key carried as the sort path); the operator's column reorder in the grid persists to `UiPreferenceStore` "report-columns:<key>", the chooser's save persists and re-renders, the export writes the visible columns in their order, and a retry repeats the last action (query or export into the previous file). `ReportsPanel.cs` supplies the query, the export (path chosen once per report) and the PII policy (`CatalogStore.GetPiiRevealPolicy().Allowed`). `ReportRunProgress.Headline` says "Sonuç hazır; dışa aktarma isteğe bağlı" once the query stages are done.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportColumnsTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): the classified column follows the policy and defaults are safe (a saved layout cannot outrank a policy that is off now); order, visibility and widths round-trip; a schema change drops a lost column and places a gained one; garbage falls back; an all-hidden layout still shows one column; `Reorder` keeps hidden columns in place and refuses a partial order; a hundred columns resolve, persist, group and search in milliseconds. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportColumnChooserUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel — the grid after a query with the default columns at schema DIP widths and the classified one hidden; the chooser driven inside its modal loop (search narrows, hide, move up, save) and the grid and the preference follow; the export writes exactly the visible columns in order; a grid reorder persists; a rebuilt panel (restart) reads the layout back; with the policy on the tracking column can be shown and every value is masked in the rows and the file; with the policy off again it disappears despite the saved layout; a hundred columns render in the chooser and search narrows them. The #847 and #848 tests moved to the query-then-export flow (the runner test now also pins the masked tracking value and the chosen-column export).

## #850 — Report empty and error states (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-850-report-result-states off codex/issue-849-report-result-columns (a80b9f4)

`ReportResultState.cs` (new): the result surface's six states from one `Compose(outcome, parameters, schema, visibleColumns)` — Ready (rows listed), TrueEmpty (the store holds no orders at all: "Bu mağazada henüz sipariş yok" → "Sipariş ekranına git"), FilteredEmpty (the store has orders the filters excluded: "Mağazada N sipariş var; seçili tarih aralığı, durum veya arama metni hiçbirini kapsamıyor." → "Son 90 güne genişlet", "Durum filtresini kaldır" only when a state filter is set, "Yeniden çalıştır"), QueryFailed (blocking: a redacted line, "Yeniden dene", "Tanılamaya git"), Cancelled (warning: "Yeniden çalıştır") and SchemaIncompatible (no visible column exists in the result's rows: "Kolonları varsayılana döndür", "Yeniden çalıştır"). `SafeUiMessage` makes the failure line fit for the screen: secrets redacted, paths ("[yol]"), SQL fragments ("[sorgu]") and connection-string parts ("[bağlantı]") stripped, one line capped at 140 characters, plain text left alone.

`ReportRunner.cs`: `ReportResult` carries `StoreTotal` (the store's order count regardless of filters, one count query, only when the result is empty) so "nothing matched" and "nothing exists" are told apart; a failed query records `"<ExceptionType>: <sanitized message>"` in the run store and the audit trail — the safe diagnostics "Tanılamaya git" leads to — while the outcome message the screen redacts further. `ReportParameterPanel.cs`: the result host gains the state surface ("report-result-state": glyph + title, text, one button per action tagged "report-result-action-<key>", severity accent and border weight, an accessible name); the grid hides for non-listed states; the primary action takes the keyboard once laid out; the actions re-query (retry), navigate (diagnostics, orders), widen the date range to the last 90 days and re-query, clear the delivery-state filter and re-query, or reset the column layout to the schema default; a cancelled or failed query also lands in the state (not only the progress rows).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportResultStateTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): each outcome's kind, severity, words and action keys (no clear-state without a state filter, no widening for a report without dates; a success without a result is a failure to show; one known column is enough for the grid; no visible column is schema-incompatible); the screen line keeps no path, secret, SQL or connection string, caps at 140 and passes plain Turkish untouched. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportResultStateUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel — listed result without a state; a state filter that matches nothing shows the filtered state with widen / clear-state / retry, the primary focused, every action a tab stop, export disabled, and clearing the state lists again; a far-past range widens to 90 days and lists; a store with no orders shows the true-empty state whose action opens the orders screen; a directory where the order database should be gives the failed state on the real path with a redacted line, retry and diagnostics (which navigates) while the audit holds "SqliteException" and the run store Failed; on the setup itself a cancelled query shows its state and its only action re-runs, and a result whose rows lack every visible column shows the schema state whose reset writes the default layout.

Not done: the store count is by shop id (a second marketplace sharing a shop id would count too); the failed-export state stays in the progress rows and status only — the result state surface covers the query, not the export; the schema-incompatible state is reachable only with a result built outside the runner (the runner's rows always match the schema), so it is exercised through the setup's context, not the real runner; the audit stores the exception type and sanitized message, not a stack trace.

## #851 — Notification center severity grouping (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-851-notification-center-grouping off codex/issue-850-report-result-states (a3b7ccb)

`NotificationStore.cs`: the dormant M145 alert ledger becomes the notification centre's owner. `LocalNotification` gains `Source`, `State` (Open/Resolved), `Occurrences`, `ResolvedUtc`, `Reopened` (additive columns, older ledgers gain them with defaults); `Sync(live, nowUtc)` reconciles the ledger with what is live now — a new fingerprint is added, a seen one repeats (a duplicate inside one batch is one more sighting, never a second alert), a resolved one that is back reopens with its acknowledgement cleared and a reopen count, an open one no longer live resolves with its time; `Acknowledge`/`Unacknowledge`; `Fingerprint(severity, source, storeKey, title)` folds case and space and ignores the detail (which changes with every count); titles and details are sanitized on write. `Add` keeps its old dedupe contract for the existing test.

`NotificationCenter.cs` (new): `Normalize` maps the board's Turkish severities ("Hata", "Uyarı", "Bilgi", "Başarılı") and the ledger's English ones onto four ranks — matched on the lower-cased word, because "Uyarı"'s dotless ı does not survive `ToUpperInvariant` (the first run of the test turned every warning into "Bilgi"); `Build(alerts, now)` groups the open, unacknowledged alerts by severity → source → store (newest first inside a group), the acknowledged ones apart in the same grouping, and the resolved ones as a capped tail with a count, with a headline ("5 açık uyarı: 3 hata, 1 uyarı, 1 bilgi · 1 onaylandı · 1 çözüldü" / "Açık uyarı yok"); `ToAlert(DashboardNotification)` turns a live finding into a ledger input (the success placeholder is not an alert); `SourceLabel` names routes and channels. `NotificationCenterPanel.Render` draws the centre: headline, an empty line, one focusable `Expander` per group (severity glyph, accent and border weight; "(count, ×sightings)"; errors and warnings open, information and acknowledged groups collapsed) with rows capped at 20 plus "… ve N daha", each row "Aç" (source route) and "Onayla"/"Geri al" with accessible names, title and detail redacted once more on screen, sighting count, relative time and "yeniden açıldı ×N"; a collapsed "Çözülenler (N)" tail.

Wired: `DashboardData.cs` — `DashboardNotification` carries a `StoreKey` (sync jobs and connection warnings name their store); `DashboardPanel.cs` — every refresh syncs the live findings into the ledger and renders the centre from it; acknowledge and undo re-render at once; the old per-row list is gone.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NotificationCenterTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first run caught the in-batch duplicate not counting, the channel label guard, and the severity casing): the ledger's repeat / acknowledgement-survives-repeat / detail-follows-latest / resolve-with-time / reopen-clears-acknowledgement rules, sanitization of a bearer token and an e-mail, `Add`'s contract, fingerprint folding and store identity; grouping order (severity, source label, store; newest first), occurrences per group, acknowledged apart, resolved tail, headlines, live-to-input mapping, source labels, and a thousand alerts grouped in milliseconds with severities never going back up. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NotificationCenterUiTests.cs` (new, 2): the real dashboard — a product out of stock becomes the "Uyarı · Ürünler (1)" group (expanded, focusable, named) beside the registry's connection warnings; its "Aç" navigates; "Onayla" moves it to the acknowledged section (collapsed, with "Geri al"), a refresh keeps it there and counts ×2; restocking resolves it into "Çözülenler (1)"; running out again reopens it unacknowledged with ×3 and "yeniden açıldı ×1"; a thousand alerts render capped per group with the cap named and no raw detail on screen; an empty centre says so.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=4; FILES_CHANGED=NotificationStore.cs, NotificationCenter.cs (new), DashboardData.cs, DashboardPanel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NotificationCenterTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NotificationCenterUiTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=583 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 579 + 4 new); artifact SHA-256 9ef44183ad1af929e053d8e31a86d0e4434b0f9aac23c3589ccb53a1e7a41fd3 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the centre lives on the dashboard only (no standalone route); the ledger grows without a retention rule (resolved alerts stay); the "Aç" of a connection alert opens the channel page, not the specific connection; the 1000-alert render is a synthetic-ledger timing, not a full dashboard with a thousand live findings; group expand/collapse state is not persisted. ## #852 — Alert snooze with expiry (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-852-alert-snooze off codex/issue-851-notification-center-grouping (2fcc63b)

`AlertSnooze.cs` (new): `AlertSnooze` (fingerprint, source, store, the severity at snooze time, until, created — never the alert's words); `AlertSnoozeRules` — a bounded duration (5 minutes to 14 days, clamped) offered as five options (1 saat … 1 hafta), `Silences(snooze, alert, now)` true only while the snooze has not ended and the alert is no more severe than when it was snoozed (a new, more severe event bypasses it; a reopen before the end stays silent — the operator asked for a time, not a sighting), `Describe` ("3 sa daha ertelendi (bitiş …)", "ertelemesi doldu"); `AlertSnoozeStore` beside the ledger in `notifications.db`: `Snooze` (upsert per fingerprint), `Clear`, `Active(now)` (drops expired rows on the way) — the table has columns for identity, scope, severity and times only.

`NotificationCenter.cs`: `Build(alerts, snoozes, now)` moves an open, unacknowledged alert a snooze silences out of the open groups into `Snoozed` (acknowledged and resolved alerts are not candidates), the headline counts "N ertelendi"; the renderer takes `snooze`/`unsnooze` callbacks: an open row gets an "Ertele" chooser (the five options, named for the screen reader, a tab stop) and the centre gains "Ertelenenler (N)" rows with the alert, its severity, the remaining time and "Ertelemeyi kaldır". `DashboardPanel.cs`: the snooze store is read on every render, a chooser pick snoozes by the ledger row's fingerprint, scope and severity, lifting clears it.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/AlertSnoozeTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): clamping and options; silence by severity and time, escalation bypass, another alert untouched, reopen before the end silent, the Turkish severity word ranking the same, the description; the centre keeping snoozed alerts apart, showing the escalated one, ignoring snoozes on acknowledged and resolved alerts, the headline, the return after the end; the store clamping, ordering by end, dropping expired rows on read, replacing on a second snooze, clearing, refusing an empty fingerprint, and the table's columns being exactly identity, scope, severity and times. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/AlertSnoozeUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real dashboard — the out-of-stock alert's row offers the chooser; "1 saat" moves the alert into "Ertelenenler (1)" with the remaining time and lifts it from the open groups, the ledger untouched, the store holding its fingerprint, source and severity; a refresh keeps the snooze while counting the sighting; a rebuilt dashboard (restart) still shows it snoozed; "Ertelemeyi kaldır" brings it back and empties the store.

## #853 — Settings navigation taxonomy (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-853-settings-taxonomy off codex/issue-852-alert-snooze (eea0ab4)

`SettingsTaxonomy.cs` (new): seven categories — Genel, Mağaza, Bağlantılar, İçe aktarma, Fiyatlandırma, Bildirimler, Tanılama — over the settings that already exist, each entry pointing at the workspace that owns it (`Route`, optional `Section` such as a channel's connection tab) or hosted inline when the settings shell itself owns it (backup, images, the local-data note); credential pages (the ten channel connection tabs and the Navlungo page) are `SecretBearing` and never inline. `Visible(routeExists)` drops an entry whose route the shell does not register and a category left empty; `ParseDeepLink`/`DeepLink` ("settings/pricing" ↔ "pricing"); `Search` over category, label and description; `ShortLabel` trims a long label for the tree (the whole label stays in the tooltip and the accessible name). No parallel settings framework: a category is links and inline hosts.

`SettingsPanel.cs` (new): the settings shell — categories down the left as named, focusable list items at a 240-DIP column (tooltip and help text carry the whole label and description), the selected category's entries on the right as rows (wrapping label and description, a "🔒 Gizli bilgiler maskeli alanlarda, kendi sayfasında düzenlenir." badge on credential pages, "Aç" that opens the owner through the section hook when there is one, inline hosts for the shell's own pieces), a search across every category that names the category on each hit and says when nothing matches, and a selector the shell uses for deep links (selects and focuses the category). `MainWindow.Navigation.cs`: the settings page is the shell (the old flat page with its per-channel buttons, image, backup and hint sections is gone — those pieces now live under their categories); `Navigate("settings/<category>")` lands on the category; a channel's connection entry opens the channel and selects its last tab.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SettingsTaxonomyTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first run caught a search fixture that matched nothing): the category set, unique keys, every entry with a label, description and route, exactly the credential pages marked and never inline, inline entries belonging to the shell, a shell without a route dropping the entry and an emptied category, deep links both ways and for unknown categories, search across categories (case-insensitive on the description), long-label trimming. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SettingsPanelUiTests.cs` (new, 2): the real shell — seven focusable named items at the DIP width, the first category open by default, the backup entry hosting the backup panel inline with no "Aç" of its own, "Aç" navigating, the connections category with its secret badges and the Etsy entry opening through the section hook, wrapping labels, search narrowing to the diagnostics entry and naming a zero result, the deep-link selector landing and focusing; a shell without the report route dropping that entry; and the real window: `Navigate("settings/pricing")` makes settings current with the pricing category selected, every visible entry points at a route the real shell registers, and an unknown category moves nothing.

## #854 — Settings section dirty-state badges (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-854-settings-dirty-badges off codex/issue-853-settings-taxonomy (1f0a8fa)

`SettingsEditState.cs` (new): one in-memory edit state for every settings form. A form gets a `FormTracker` for its taxonomy entry key and registers fields with a getter (`Track(label, current, secret)`); `Snapshot` after load and after a successful save makes the current values the baseline; `Recompute` on every edit reports a `DirtySection` naming the changed fields by label ("Kaydedilmemiş değişiklik: KDV %, Tarih"). A secret field's getter is reduced to presence before it is compared or stored, so a changed secret reads "API secret (gizli)" and its value can reach neither the state nor a badge, a tooltip or a log; `Changed` fires on transitions only; `All` orders sections by when they went dirty; `Clear` resets a section; a getter that throws reads as empty. Being in memory, a restart without a save starts clean — as the forms do.

Wired into two real owners: `LocaleSettingsPanel.cs` (Kanal, Mağaza, Döviz, Kültür, KDV %, Tarih; snapshot on open, after Kaydet and after "Seçileni yükle"; its errors now land in the status line instead of a modal) and `TrendyolPanel.cs` (Supplier ID, API key, API secret by presence, User-Agent; snapshot after load and after "Şifreli kaydet"). `SettingsPanel.cs`: `Context.EditState`; a category whose entries have unsaved changes carries "●" after its label, its tooltip lists each dirty entry's field labels and its accessible name says "kaydedilmemiş değişiklik"; an entry row shows the mark and a badge line with the summary; the shell re-renders on `Changed`. `MainWindow.Navigation.cs` owns the one state and hands it to the two forms and the shell.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SettingsEditStateTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): labels only, secrets by presence and "(gizli)", event on transitions only, ordering by dirty time with the first moment kept, back-to-saved is clean, clear, a cleared secret is a change, one tracker per key, refused empty keys and labels, a throwing getter, a fresh state clean. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SettingsDirtyUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real locale and Trendyol forms and the real shell sharing one state — editing the VAT marks Genel and the locale entry with "KDV %" (tooltip and accessible name too) and nothing else; typing a Trendyol secret marks Bağlantılar with "API secret (gizli)" while the value appears in no text and no tooltip of the shell; the marks survive switching categories and a deep link; a save blocked by validation ("KDV oranı … sayı olmalı") keeps the mark; a valid save clears it while the other section stays pending; a rebuilt shell and forms with a fresh state (a restart without saving) show no mark, the saved VAT and no Trendyol file.

## #855 — Connection secret masking controls (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-855-secret-masking off codex/issue-854-settings-dirty-badges (8288e94)

`SecretField.cs` (new): the secret input standard, built on the form-row standard (#819). `SecretField.Build(label, help, required)` returns a `SecretFieldControl` — the labelled row, the `PasswordBox`, the presence line. Masked by construction (a PasswordBox; there is no reveal); copy and cut are refused on the box (preview CanExecute false, preview Executed handled); a paste is cleaned to one line by `Clean` (CR dropped, the first non-empty trimmed line, surrounding quotes removed, capped at 512 characters — a key copied with a trailing newline or a quote is the classic failure) and set through `Password`, so the form's own change tracking runs; a non-text paste is refused. Presence: `SetSaved(present)` says only "Kayıtlı gizli değer var…" / "Kayıtlı gizli değer yok." under the help text and in the box's accessible help — the saved value is never loaded into the box; `Resolve(saved)` gives the typed value, or the saved one when the box was left alone, so an empty box on save keeps the stored secret instead of overwriting it; `MarkSaved` empties the box after a save and `MarkCleared` after a delete. What is typed flows to the store and nowhere else.

Wired into the real connection forms. `TrendyolPanel.cs` (API secret): a save with nothing typed and nothing saved is refused in the row's own validation slot and the status line — worded "Gizli değer gerekli." without the literal word "secret", which the redaction pass blanks (the first test run showed "API [redacted] gerekli."); a validation failure keeps what was typed and writes nothing; the store follows the shell's data directory. `EbayPanel.cs` (Cert ID) and `OzonPanel.cs` (API key): `Create(directory, editState)`, the stores at the shell's `ebay.bin`/`ozon.bin`, presence from the saved connection instead of the secret in the box, `Resolve` on read (eBay keeps its OAuth tokens when the settings, resolved secret included, are unchanged), `MarkSaved` after a save, `MarkCleared` on delete, and the #854 edit tracker with the secret by presence. `MarketplaceSetupPanel.CreateChannel(id, directory, editState)` and `MainWindow.Navigation.cs` pass the shell's directory and edit state, so the eBay and Ozon tabs use the app's stores and tests never touch the profile's files.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SecretFieldTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): the paste cleaning cases; in a window — a PasswordBox that is a tab stop with the accessible name "API secret, zorunlu", the presence texts, typed-or-saved resolution, Copy and Cut refused and Paste allowed on the focused box, a validation failure keeping the typed value, MarkSaved/MarkCleared, and no TextBlock on the row ever carrying a value. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SecretPresenceUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real Trendyol and Ozon forms in a temporary directory — an empty form says no saved value; a save with no secret is refused in the slot and the status with nothing written; a validation failure ("sayısal olmalı") keeps the typed secret and writes nothing; a valid save encrypts it, empties the box, shows presence and puts the value in no text; a rebuilt form shows presence, an empty box and the value nowhere, a save with the box left empty keeps the stored secret, and a typed value replaces it; the Ozon form the same, plus a delete clearing presence; every box a labelled tab stop.

Not done: six connection forms (Amazon, Allegro, Fruugo, Hepsiburada, Joom, Wish) still use a plain PasswordBox that loads the saved secret into the box, and the XML source password field is untouched — each needs the same swap; eBay's one-shot OAuth return-URL box (cleared after use) stays a plain PasswordBox; the eBay form is wired but exercised only through the shared standard and the build, not its own window test; the unused overview `MarketplaceSetupPanel.Create()` still builds the eBay/Ozon panels on the profile-default store; there is no reveal control by design, so an operator cannot verify a typed value before saving except by saving.

#855 fix-up (2026-09-13, on the same branch): the first CI run was red on `SecretFieldTests` — `ApplicationCommands.Paste.CanExecute(null, box)` is answered from the machine's clipboard (text on the dev machine, nothing on the CI runner), so it never said anything about the row. The assertion is replaced by raising `DataObject.PastingEvent` on the box with a quoted, multi-line `DataObject`: the cleaned single line lands in the box with the default paste cancelled, and a non-text (file) paste is cancelled and changes nothing — the paste-cleaning path is now exercised end to end instead of a clipboard probe. TEST_RESULT=595 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release, local); the CI run is re-checked on this commit.

## #856 — Settings validation banner (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-856-settings-validation-banner off codex/issue-855-secret-masking (27f060e)

`SettingsValidation.cs` (new): the model — `SettingsIssue(Kind, EntryKey, Title, Detail, Severity)` with kinds InvalidConfig / MissingSecret / StaleConnection / SaveConflict, each tied to the taxonomy entry that owns the fix. `Evaluate(connections, locales, reported, nowUtc)` works on facts, never values: a connection record that fails its own rules or cannot be read is invalid configuration (blocking); a record whose secret is empty, or credentials gone after the registry verified the connection once, is a missing secret (blocking) — never set up is not an issue; a failed last test (with the stored, redacted error) or a verification older than 30 days is a stale connection (warning); credentials never verified are information; a form's reported conflict rides along. Disabled connections, policy-blocked ones and channels without a settings entry are skipped. Blocking first, then warnings, then information; inside a level the taxonomy's order; duplicates folded; every text through the uncapped redaction, the raw-payload check and a 200-character cap. `Headline` ("3 ayar sorunu: 2 engelleyici, 1 uyarı" / "Ayar sorunu yok"). `Collect(directory, editState, nowUtc)` probes the real stores: the registry's rows per channel, the nine credential files (present? secret non-empty? `Load` failing with a validation message or unreadable), every locale row against `LocaleSettings.Validate`, plus the edit state's reported issues. `SettingsValidationBanner.Render` draws the banner: headline, one focusable row per issue (severity glyph and word, title, the owning entry's label, the detail; "Git" opens the owner; Enter on the row too), "Yeniden denetle" and "Kapat"; Escape inside dismisses until the next check; when the previous check's issues are all gone one success line says so; DIP paddings and wrapping text so a narrow or scaled window grows it instead of clipping.

`SettingsEditState.cs`: `Report(issue)` / `Resolve(kind, key)` / `Reported` for what the forms find on their own path, `NotifySaved(key)` after a store write (clears that section's conflict), events `IssuesChanged` and `Saved`. `SettingsPanel.cs`: `Context.Validate`; the banner host under the title; the check runs on open, after every announced save and on every reported or resolved conflict; a row's "Git" selects the owning category and opens the owner through the section hook (a channel's connection tab) or the route. `LocaleSettingsPanel.cs`: the form remembers the row and version it loaded or last saved; a save over a newer version of that row (saved elsewhere meanwhile) reloads the grid, reports the conflict with the versions and refuses to write; "Seçileni yükle" resolves it; a successful save announces itself. `TrendyolPanel.cs`, `EbayPanel.cs`, `OzonPanel.cs`: a save or a delete announces itself. `MainWindow.Navigation.cs`: the shell's check is `SettingsValidation.Collect` over the app's data directory and edit state.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SettingsValidationTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): a fresh install has no issue; the rules on eleven fact rows (missing secret two ways, invalid with a redacted specimen, 45-day stale, failed with a redacted bearer, never verified, healthy, disabled, policy-blocked, unknown channel), a locale failure, a duplicated reported conflict — order, kinds, severities, headline, capped detail; the edit state's report / latest-wins / resolve / notify-saved contract; the probe over a real directory (never verified → information, a 40-day-old verification → warning, credentials deleted after a verification → blocking, an unreadable credential file → invalid without its bytes, a locale row with an unsupported currency → invalid on the locale entry, a reported conflict alongside, no stored value in any text). `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SettingsValidationUiTests.cs` (new, 1 — the first run caught a text check reading `TextBlock.Text` where the row is built from runs): the real shell and the real Trendyol and locale forms over a temporary directory — two blocking rows in taxonomy order, focusable, named, wrapping; the banner and its lines inside the shell at 520 DIP; "Git" selecting the connections category and opening trendyol/connection, the locale link navigating; saving credentials on the real Trendyol form re-checks and drops the row with the typed values nowhere in the shell; a save conflict produced on the real locale form (the row saved elsewhere between load and save) refused with nothing written, on the banner with the versions; Escape dismissing; reloading the row resolving it with the success line once; a clean save afterwards leaving no banner.

## #857 — Resource token consolidation (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-857-design-tokens off codex/issue-856-settings-validation-banner (321c310)

`Themes/DesignTokens.xaml` (new): one resource dictionary with the semantic tokens every screen shares — a spacing scale (SpaceHairline 2, SpaceInline 4, SpaceControl 8, SpaceSection 12, SpacePage 20), control paddings and margins (ControlMargin 3; InputPadding 7,5; ButtonPadding 13,8; CompactButtonPadding 10,5; CardPadding 10; HeaderPadding 8,10; TabPadding 16,9; NavigationItemPadding 14,9; NavigationItemMargin 8,2), control heights (ControlMinHeight 30, RowHeight 36), borders (BorderHairline 1, BorderEmphasis 2) and radii (CardRadius 6, ShellRadius 7) — typed as `sys:Double`, `Thickness` and `CornerRadius`. Every value is the device-independent number the styles and panels used before, so nothing moves on screen. No parallel theme framework: it is the existing ResourceDictionary structure, filled.

`App.xaml`: the application's resources merge the token file (the app resource merge — every window and dialog resolves against it). `MainWindow.xaml`: the window merges the same file itself, so a window built by a test host or a tool (no Application) resolves the same values; every implicit style (Button, TextBox, PasswordBox, ComboBox, DataGrid, DataGridColumnHeader, GroupBox, TabItem, the NavigationItem style) and the shell's radii and the sidebar search height now read `{StaticResource …}` tokens instead of literals. `DesignTokens.cs` (new): the accessor the code-built panels read — the running application's resources when they carry the tokens, otherwise the token file loaded as a component — with typed properties per token and `Required` (key + type); `Verify(resources)` throws `InvalidOperationException` naming every missing or mistyped key. `App.xaml.cs`: `OnStartup` runs `VerifyDesignTokens(Resources)` after the crash handlers are in place, so a missing token is a startup failure with the key in the crash log, never a control quietly falling back to a default. `FormField.cs`: the row's label gap and row gap come from the tokens (the help gap stays its own 3 DIP). Migrated by value: the 16 page roots that used `new Thickness(20)` (settings shell, reports, diagnostics, media, order exceptions, readiness, taxonomy, sync, automation, Excel, price and stock policies, product editing, channel matrix, marketplace setup, Etsy readiness) read `SpacePage`; the seven connection forms' compact button padding reads `CompactButtonPadding`.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/DesignTokensTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): the dictionary carries every required key with its type and no duplicate; the values are the numbers the screens used (visual regression by value); a copy missing a key, a copy with a mistyped key and an empty dictionary all fail verification naming the keys; the app's startup check is the same verification, and a dictionary that merges the token file (as App.xaml's does) passes. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/DesignTokensUiTests.cs` (new, 1 — the first runs caught the product list's deliberate density row height and the channel page's unselected connection tab): the real main window with no Application resolves `SpacePage` from its own merge; every implicit style's padding, margin, min-height and row height equals the token; the header's real button and search box, the sidebar search height, a settings grid with no row height of its own, the settings shell's page margin and the Trendyol form's compact button measure with the tokens; a form row's rhythm reads them; the product list's row height is a deliberate local choice; the window's DPI scale does not enter the numbers.

## #858 — Typography scale tokens (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-858-typography-tokens off codex/issue-857-design-tokens (ddc05b8)

`Themes/DesignTokens.xaml`: the typography scale joins the token file — one face for text (`FontFamilyBody` Segoe UI) and one for code and diagnostics (`FontFamilyMono` Consolas, Courier New), the title and KPI weights (SemiBold, Bold), and the role sizes: page title 25, section title 20, subsection title 16, body 13, caption 11, numeric KPI 25, monospace 13 — plus the shared text styles for XAML (`PageTitleText`, `SectionTitleText`, `SubsectionTitleText`, `BodyText`, `CaptionText`, `KpiText`), all wrapping. `DesignTokens.cs`: the eleven keys are required at startup and exposed as typed properties. `TextStyles.cs` (new): `TextRole` (PageTitle, SectionTitle, SubsectionTitle, Body, Hint, Caption, Kpi, Mono); `Apply(block, role, highContrast?)` sets size, weight and face, wraps when the block has no say, and gives the two muted roles the design grey — or the system's grey-text colour under high contrast, so a hint never vanishes on a black ground — while a caller's text, margin and colour stay; `Create(role, text)`; `ApplyMono(input)` for a code or diagnostics box. The numbers are device-independent: a 200 % display scales the host, not the tokens.

Screens: `MainWindow.xaml` — the header's page title and breadcrumb use the shared styles (the title now wraps a long Turkish label instead of clipping it); `MainWindow.xaml.cs` — the window's base face and size come from the tokens after `InitializeComponent` (a root element's attributes cannot reference the window's own resources), the pricing formula box takes the monospace role, and the shared `Heading`/`Hint` helpers apply the section-title and hint roles. The same two helpers in the channel matrix, diagnostics, media, order exceptions, readiness, reports and settings panels, and the `Text(value, role)` helper of the API health, bulk products, data quality, locale, message, migration, onboarding, orders and policy-centre panels, read the roles (their callers' numeric sizes 22/24/21 fold into the section role, 18 into the subsection role, 12 into body). One-off titles (the eight channel and dashboard titles at 22, subsection titles at 16 and 18, the KPI figure at 25 Bold), captions (10 and 11) and the navigation group labels read the tokens directly. Net visual change, deliberate: section titles 22→20, the onboarding and policy-centre titles 24/21→20, detail titles 18→16, panel body copy 12→13, fine print 10→11.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/TextStylesTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): every role's size, weight, face and wrapping from the tokens; `Apply` leaving text, margin and a caller's colour alone; the muted roles' design grey and, under high contrast, the system grey; body inheriting the surface's colour; the monospace input; a 100-character Turkish label wrapping to more than one line inside a 320-DIP host for every role; the same text measuring the same DIP height under a 2× layout scale while the host doubles. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/TextStylesUiTests.cs` (new, 1 — the first runs caught the sidebar carrying the same titles at body size, the dashboard's asynchronous cards and the formula box on the XML page's third tab): the real main window — base face and size, the header title and breadcrumb, the dashboard's section title and a KPI figure, the settings shell's title and hint colour, the Trendyol form's title, the formula box's face and size, and a long Turkish page title wrapping inside its column at the window's minimum width.

## #859 — Spacing scale tokens (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-859-spacing-scale off codex/issue-858-typography-tokens (5dd209b)

`Themes/DesignTokens.xaml`: the four-step scale (inline 4, control 8, section 12, page 20, with the 2-DIP hairline) becomes the only source of the layout rhythm — the page, section and inline margins as `Thickness` tokens (`PageMargin`, `SectionMargin`, `InlineMargin`), the chip padding (`ChipPadding` 8,2) and the three text-block rhythms the shared helpers use (`TitleBlockMargin` 4,8,4,12; `HintBlockMargin` 4,8,4,8; `BodyBlockMargin` 3,4,3,7), plus the shared layout styles for XAML (`PageRoot`, `SectionRoot` for any panel). `DesignTokens.cs`: the seven keys are required at startup and exposed. `Spacing.cs` (new): `Page`, `Section`, `Inline`, `Control` (the 3-DIP control margin), `Chip`, `TitleBlock`, `HintBlock`, `BodyBlock`, and the directional gaps built from the scale — `BelowSection`, `BelowControl`, `BelowInline`, `AboveInline`, `AboveControl`, `VerticalControl`, `RightInline`, `LeftControl` — so a panel says what a gap is for, never how many pixels it is. Device-independent throughout: a 200 % display scales the host, not the rhythm.

Migration, controlled and by value: across 48 panels and dialogs the recurring literals became the roles with the same numbers — `(12)`→Section (20 sites), `(4)`→Inline (40), `(3)`→Control (36), `(8,2,8,2)`→Chip (19), `(0,0,0,8)`→BelowControl (16), `(0,0,0,12)`→BelowSection (15), `(0,4,0,0)`→AboveInline (15), `(0,8,0,8)`→VerticalControl (14), `(0,0,0,4)`→BelowInline (14), the heading/hint/body helper rhythms→TitleBlock/HintBlock/BodyBlock (33), `(0,8,0,0)`/`(0,0,4,0)`/`(8,0,0,0)`→AboveControl/RightInline/LeftControl (11); the one deliberate change: the nine channel-form and onboarding roots move from their own 18 to the page margin (20), so every page opens with the same edge.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SpacingTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): the seven tokens present with their type; every `Spacing` value; each derived from the scale it names (a below-section gap's bottom is the section step, the chip's sides the control step and its top the hairline); a page keeping its 20-DIP margin under a 2× layout scale while the host doubles. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SpacingUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real main window on the five screens the issue names — the settings shell's page margin and its entries' control rhythm, the orders root's section margin, the dashboard's KPI cards' inline margin (after their asynchronous load), the Trendyol form's root on the page margin, the products and XML screens carrying the scale on their blocks, and at the window's minimum width none of the five pages growing wider than the content area.

## #860 — Icon sizing and alignment tokens (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-860-icon-tokens off codex/issue-859-spacing-scale (3427219)

The app draws its icons as text glyphs (severity marks, the sidebar toggle, Back, close, the column-chooser arrows, the import stepper's stage marks, the collapsed navigation rail's initials), so the standard is a size per role and the text's own baseline. `Themes/DesignTokens.xaml`: `IconSizeInline` 13, `IconSizeToolbar` 16, `IconSizeNavigation` 14, `IconButtonMinSize` 26, `ToolbarButtonMinSize` 32 — required at startup and exposed by `DesignTokens.cs`. `IconStyles.cs` (new): `IconRole` (Inline, Status, Toolbar, Navigation); `Size(role)`, `ButtonMinSize(role)`; `GlyphRun(glyph, role, brush?)` — a run sized for its role that sits on the baseline of the text it belongs to; `WithText(glyph, text, role)` — glyph and label in one block, one baseline by construction, the label at the block's size; `Glyph(glyph, role)` — a glyph alone, centred in a square of its size; `ApplyIconButton(button, role, name?)` — a square minimum with centred content, the role's glyph size and compact padding when the content is a glyph alone (which then must have a spoken name, or the call is refused), a mixed glyph-and-label button keeping its body size and padding, and a dimmed disabled state (opacity 0.55) that follows `IsEnabled`. `MainWindow.xaml`: a `ToolbarIconButton` style based on the app's button (square, centred, the toolbar glyph size) on the sidebar toggle.

Wired: the Back button (`‹ Geri`, dimmed while it cannot act), the toast's close button (inline, named) and the toast's glyph run, the import stepper's stage chips (status role; stages that cannot be jumped to read as disabled), the column chooser's ▲/▼ buttons, the error banner's and the settings banner's glyph runs, and the collapsed navigation rail (the initials at the navigation size, the body size when expanded).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/IconStylesTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): the five tokens with their type and values; sizes and minimums per role; a glyph-and-label block's runs (glyph sized and baseline-aligned, label at the block's size), a lone glyph run, a centred glyph; an icon button's square, alignment, glyph size and padding with the caller's string kept; a named toolbar button; an icon-only button without a name refused while a mixed one passes; disabled dimming and recovery; the square staying 26 DIP under a 2× layout scale while the host doubles. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/IconStylesUiTests.cs` (new, 1 — the first run caught that the stepper draws its chips only once a source is selected): the real main window — the toggle's square, size, alignment and name; collapsing the rail printing one- or two-character glyphs at the navigation size and expanding restoring the body size; the Back button as a mixed icon button dimmed while disabled; a toast raised through the window's own log with an inline, named close button and a glyph run on the text's baseline; the import stepper's chips as icon buttons with disabled stages dimmed.

## #861 — Focus visual tokens (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-861-focus-visuals off codex/issue-860-icon-tokens (f206d9f)

One keyboard focus ring for every control. `Themes/DesignTokens.xaml`: `FocusRingColor` (#173646), `FocusRingInnerColor` (white), `FocusRingThickness` 2, `FocusRingInnerThickness` 1, `FocusRingMargin` −2, the two brushes, and the shared `KeyboardFocusVisual` style — a control template drawing a dark outer stroke and a light inner stroke just outside the control's bounds (so the ring reads on a teal button, a white page and the dark navigation rail alike), pixel-snapped and never hit-testable, with a trigger that swaps to the system's text and window colours under high contrast. `DesignTokens.cs`: the five keys required at startup and exposed. `FocusStyles.cs` (new): `Create(highContrast?)` builds the same ring in code, a fresh sealed template per call, because a Style belongs to the thread that made it and the cached token dictionary's copy is never handed out; `Apply(element)`, `MakeFocusable(element)` (a code-built row or box becomes a keyboard stop with the ring), `AddTo(style)` (a code-built grid row or cell style takes it). WPF draws a `FocusVisualStyle` only when focus arrived by keyboard, so the mouse keeps the control's own state and the keyboard gets the ring; a disabled control cannot take focus at all.

`MainWindow.xaml`: every shared control style — Button, TextBox, PasswordBox, ComboBox, CheckBox, TabItem, the navigation item — names the ring, and new implicit DataGridRow and DataGridCell styles give grid rows and cells the same ring. In code: the settings banner rows, the field-diff rows and the orders screen's customer, returns and return-request boxes become keyboard stops with the ring; the product grid's density cell style, the channel matrix's cell styles, the dashboard's connection-row style and the XML mapping row style carry it too.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FocusStylesTests.cs` (new, 1, red first — the first run caught that a code-built template must be sealed before it can be instantiated): the tokens with their types and values; the token file carrying the style; the built ring's two strokes with the token thickness, colours and outside margin, pixel-snapped, not hit-testable, unfilled; the high-contrast build using the system's text and window colours; a code-built border becoming a focusable tab stop with the ring, a disabled button refusing focus, a focusable row taking keyboard focus in a window; a settings banner row carrying the ring. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FocusStylesUiTests.cs` (new, 1 — the first run showed that a synthetic key event does not register as WPF's last input device, only real input reports do): the real main window — the ring style named by all eight implicit styles and the navigation item and resolved on the header's real search box and button; with the keyboard stated as the last input device, a Tab through the input pipeline moves keyboard focus and the ring adorner appears on the new element; with the mouse as the last device, focus keeps the control focused with no ring; the disabled Back button refuses focus; the stroke stays the token's DIP value whatever the window's scale.

## #862 — Contrast audit for semantic states (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-862-contrast-audit off codex/issue-861-focus-visuals (9ab5350)

`ContrastAudit.cs` (new): WCAG 2.x relative luminance, contrast ratio and an opacity blend, thresholds by use (`ContrastKind`: text 4.5:1, large text 3:1, a border or icon 3:1; a disabled control and a pair the operating system owns are measured and reported, never failed — the normal Windows highlight is 4.46:1 by the system's own choice), and a catalogue of the pairs the main screens draw, read from the tokens and the severity table so a colour that drifts is caught: the page title, body, muted and secondary text on white; the warning status text; the button's label on the button and its edge on the page; the navigation rail's items, selected item and subtitle; the selected row's text on its highlight, its rule on white and the unfocused selection; the focus ring on the light ground, the dark rail and the teal button; for each severity the accent as text and as a border on its own surface, as an icon on white, and the toast text on the surface; the disabled button and disabled text. Under high contrast: the system's text, highlight and control pairs and the severities' system accents as emphasis. `Audit(highContrast)` returns every finding with its ratio, its requirement and whether it passes.

The audit found and the tokens fix: the help and caption grey (126,146,158) at 3.2:1 and the breadcrumb grey (#718795) at 3.8:1 become the muted token (87,112,125, 5.2:1); the orange status text (DarkOrange, 2.3:1) on the eBay, Navlungo and marketplace-setup forms becomes the warning text token (160,82,22, 5.7:1); the amber (196,132,22, 3.2:1) on the dashboard's stale cards and the thin-margin price summary becomes the warning accent; the row-state badge colours for a disabled product (4.0:1 → 5.4:1) and a stale one (3.9:1 → 6.3:1) darken; the media preview glyph (2.8:1 → 4.1:1) darkens; the search-result secondary line's grey becomes the muted token; the selected row is the token highlight (27,79,114) with white text (8.7:1), the quiet inactive highlight (214,226,235) with the primary text when the grid has no focus, and a 3-DIP rule on the row's edge, so a selection is never colour alone. `Themes/DesignTokens.xaml`: the semantic colour tokens (`TextPrimaryColor`, `TextMutedColor`, `TextSecondaryColor`, `WarningTextColor`, `AccentColor`, `AccentForegroundColor`, `PageBackgroundColor`, `SurfaceColor`, the four rail colours, the three selected-row colours, the rule) and their brushes; `DesignTokens.cs` exposes them. `MainWindow.xaml`: the implicit DataGridRow and DataGridCell styles carry the selection triggers, and the header's breadcrumb, title and description read the brushes. `RowSelection.cs` (new): the same triggers for code-built row and cell styles (the product grid's density cell style, the channel matrix's cell styles, the dashboard's connection-row style, the XML mapping row style). `TextStyles.MutedColor` and `FormField`'s help brush read the token.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ContrastAuditTests.cs` (new, 1, red first — the first run caught the system's own highlight pair at 4.46:1, now reported as system-owned): the ratio math against 21:1, 1:1 and the #767676 boundary; the blend; the thresholds; the fixed tokens each at 4.5:1; every catalogued pair passing (34 pairs) with text and non-text pairs for every severity, disabled pairs measured and exempt, the selected row, the focus ring and the rail present; the high-contrast catalogue passing with the system pairs reported; every severity carrying a glyph and a word; the selected row's rule. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ContrastAuditUiTests.cs` (new, 1 — the first run showed the product grid's rows own their border for the row-state rule, so the selection rule is checked on a grid without state borders): the real main window — the breadcrumb on the muted token; no text block on the dashboard, products, orders, XML, settings, Trendyol or eBay screens drawing the old help grey, the old breadcrumb grey, the orange or the amber; a form row's help text and a settings hint on the muted token; the eBay status line on the warning token; a selected product row's cell on the token highlight with readable text; a selected connection row on the dashboard with the highlight and the rule.

## #863 — Form control height consistency (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-863-form-control-heights off codex/issue-862-contrast-audit (3d983c9)

One standard for the form controls, in the token file so every window and dialog resolves it. `Themes/DesignTokens.xaml`: `HitTargetMinSize` 24 (the pointer-target minimum) beside `ControlMinHeight` 30, the accent brushes, and the shared implicit control styles moved here from the main window — an input (TextBox, PasswordBox, ComboBox and, new, DatePicker) stands at least the control height with the input padding and the control margin; a Button is at least the hit target on both sides (its label padding keeps form buttons taller) and reads the accent brushes; a CheckBox is at least the hit target with its content centred; the DataGrid, its header, rows and cells, GroupBox and TabItem styles come along unchanged; every one carries the keyboard focus ring. `MainWindow.xaml` keeps only what is the window's own (the token merge, the toolbar icon button, the navigation item); `DialogLayout.cs`: `DialogShell.Create` merges the token dictionary into the dialog window, so a dialog resolves the same tokens and styles with or without an Application (before, a dialog's inputs were the theme's 22-DIP defaults beside the window's 30). `DesignTokens.cs` exposes the new token. A long value never changes a single-line input's height: the box scrolls, a label wraps. The numbers are device-independent.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FormControlStylesTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): the token; the token file carrying the six control styles with their minimum heights (inputs 30, button and check box 24) and the ring; the date picker's padding and the check box's alignment; a dialog built by the shell with a text box, a combo box, a check box, a date picker and a button — every one at its minimum with no Application; a 400-character value leaving the box's height unchanged; the height staying a DIP number under a 2× layout scale while the host doubles. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FormControlStylesUiTests.cs` (new, 1 — the first run caught the date picker's inner text box, a template part, at 24: the control is what must stand at the height, so template parts are the control's own business): the real main window — the product card, the XML source form and the locale settings form with every visible input at the control height and every button and check box at the hit target; Tab walking the locale form's inputs and buttons in order, each a new stop; a long Turkish value keeping the input's height; the report setup's date pickers at the control height where present.
