Not done: the metrics are written per boundary on the UI thread (one small SQLite insert each; a busy disk adds its cost to the screen rather than a background queue); the other screens (sync centre, reports, channel matrix, settings) are not instrumented; the import apply stage (`ImportAsync`) is measured by the catalog store's own import measurement, not here; the diagnostics page shows only the check line, not a per-view table or trend; the summary is over the retained rows (5000) with no time window; the orders page's list filter (`Filter()`) is not a measured refresh; no threshold alerts the operator beyond the WARN line.

## #879 — UI freeze watchdog diagnostics (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-879-freeze-watchdog off codex/issue-878-ui-latency (1add49c)

A UI thread that stops answering is a diagnostic event with a duration, the active command and a correlation id — never a stack or a payload. `UiFreezeWatchdog.cs` (new): `UiActivity` is the registry of logical UI operations in flight — `Enter(name, correlation?)` returns a token to dispose, names are identifiers only (`Safe` maps anything else to "unnamed"; `NameOf(delegate)` gives a method's name cleaned to identifier characters), `Current` is the innermost. `UiFreezeWatchdog.Start(dispatcher, audit, thresholdMs, intervalMs, onFreeze?)` runs a background thread that posts a heartbeat to the dispatcher at Normal priority every 250 ms (default threshold 1500 ms, floor 100 ms); when a heartbeat stays unanswered past the threshold the UI thread is frozen and the active command and its correlation are captured at that moment; when the heartbeat finally runs the freeze is recorded once — `UiFreezeEvent(AtUtc, DurationMs, ActiveCommand, Correlation)` kept in memory (last 100) and an audit row (module "ui", action "freeze", outcome WARN, detail `Describe` through the sanitizer: "UI N ms yanıt vermedi · etkin komut: X · korelasyon: Y"). False-positive guards: legitimate long work that awaits (Task.Run, Task.Delay) never blocks a Normal-priority heartbeat and is never reported; the first heartbeat after start is a warm-up and not measured; an overdue heartbeat while a debugger is attached is a breakpoint, not a freeze; a dispatcher that is shutting down ends the watch; recovery clears `IsFrozen` and a second freeze is a second event. `Check(events?, threshold)` is the diagnostics line "UI donması" (a note that nothing is watching when there is no watchdog in the process, OK with none, WARN with the count, the longest and the last command); `Current`/`Install` hold the process-wide instance. Wired: `App.OnStartup` starts and installs it on the application dispatcher through `App.StartWatchdog(dispatcher, directory, threshold, interval)` (the audit store on the data directory; in memory only when the store cannot open) and `OnExit` disposes it; the shell's `RunAsync` and `RunGuarded` enter the delegate's method name as the activity for the whole operation, `HandleShortcut` enters the matched command key; a `LatencyScope` (#878) enters "<view>-<phase>" with its own correlation for its lifetime, so a freeze during a slow load shares the load's id; `DiagnosticsService.Build` adds the check (the support package carries it through diagnostics.json and the freeze rows through audit.json).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/UiFreezeWatchdogTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): on a real dispatcher thread with a 200 ms threshold and 25 ms heartbeats — an idle dispatcher reports nothing; 600 ms of awaited work on the UI thread reports nothing; a 700 ms block inside a named command with a given correlation is recorded once after recovery (duration ≥ 450 ms, the command and the correlation, the callback once, the audit row WARN with the command, the correlation, "ms" and no stack frame); a second block outside any command is a second event naming idle with a new correlation, and still only two after settling; the diagnostics line WARN with "2 donma" and idle, OK for none, "izlenmiyor" for no watchdog; activities nest and the innermost is current, a named delegate gives its name, a lambda an identifier, a non-identifier "unnamed", `Describe` never carries a non-identifier command. The application entry: `App.StartWatchdog` installs the current watchdog, the diagnostics read OK then WARN "1 donma" after a 600 ms block, the audit row lands in the data directory, disposing uninstalls and the diagnostics say "izlenmiyor".

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=UiFreezeWatchdog.cs (new), App.xaml.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, MainWindow.UX.cs, UiLatency.cs, AuditStore.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/UiFreezeWatchdogTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=649 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 647 + 2 new); artifact SHA-256 0ce8bc5b0d1442937b4d011cd657ca4ee7cdc646a3bf05755688f8ad1c9ead12 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the active command is the innermost logical operation in flight (a guarded command stays "active" across its awaits, so a freeze caused by something else while it waits is attributed to it as the context, not as the culprit); button clicks that do not go through `RunAsync`/`RunGuarded`/a shortcut are idle to the watchdog; the freeze rows are audit rows (module "ui") rather than a store of their own, so they share the audit retention; a freeze longer than the watchdog's thread can observe (the process suspended, a laptop asleep) is measured with the monotonic clock as one long freeze; there is no user-facing notice at the moment of recovery, only the diagnostics line and the audit trail; the threshold is a constant (1500 ms), not a setting.

## #880 — Collision-safe filename generation (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-880-export-filenames off codex/issue-879-freeze-watchdog (cf49348)

Every export suggests a sanitized, timestamped, duplicate-safe file name, and no export replaces an existing file silently. `ExportFileNames.cs` (new): `SafeStem(name)` keeps letters and digits, turns separators, control characters and the characters a file system refuses into dashes, collapses and trims them, prefixes a reserved device name (CON, PRN, AUX, NUL, COM1–9, LPT1–9) with "x-", caps the stem at 60 characters, and falls back to the generic "disa-aktarim" when the name is empty or when the central redaction would change it — an e-mail, a phone number or a secret never reaches a file name; `SafeExtension` is lower-case letters and digits (at most eight, "dat" when nothing is left); `Build(name, extension, at?)` is "stem-yyyyMMdd-HHmmss.ext" to the second; `Unique(path)` returns the path when free and otherwise the first "-2", "-3"… variant before the extension; `Suggest(directory, name, extension, at?)` combines them. `ExportFiles.Commit(temporary, path, overwrite)` is the one way an export lands on its final path: an existing file is replaced only when the caller asked (`ExportFileExistsException`, an `IOException` whose message "Dosya zaten var; üzerine yazılmadı: <file>" names the file through the redaction and never its directory, is thrown otherwise, and the temporary file stays the caller's to remove). Wired at every export writer and dialog: `ReportRunner.ExportAsync` gained `overwrite` (default false; the failure surfaces as the run's Failed outcome and its recorded message) and `ReportsPanel` passes true, because its consent is already given — the standard save dialog confirmed any replacement (an injected `choosePath` stands in for that dialog, confirmation included) or the run is a retry rewriting its own file; the report dialog suggests `Build(definition.Key, "csv")` instead of a minute-precision name; `RejectedRowsExport.WriteCsv`, `CatalogExcel.ExportErrors` and `CatalogExcel.Export` gained `overwrite` and commit through the seam, the Excel page passes true after its dialogs and suggests "reddedilen-satirlar-…xlsx" / "urunler-…xlsx"; `SupportPackageService.Export` and `DataBackupService.Backup` gained `overwrite`, the diagnostics and backup pages pass true after their dialogs and suggest "monobridge-destek-…zip" / "monobridge-yedek-…zip" (the backup used to suggest a minute-precision name that collided within the minute); the product XML export now writes a temporary file and commits it (it used to write the final path directly; the one-parameter `ExportProductsToXmlFileAsync` test seam keeps its shape and delegates to `WriteProductsXmlAsync(path, overwrite)`) with the dialog's confirmation, and the XML template export suggests a timestamped name.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ExportFileNamesTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): sanitizing (a Turkish report title with a colon, a slash and a question mark; a tab and a control character; only invalid characters; "con" and "LPT1"; a 200-character name; blank and null; an e-mail and a "password=" value falling back), extensions, a timestamp to the second, same-second suggestions "-2" and "-3" in a real directory, a free path returned as it is, committing a new file, the refusal of an existing one (the file intact, the temporary kept, the message with the file name and without the directory) and the explicit replacement; the writers on real paths — the rejected-rows CSV twice (a failed result naming the file, then success with `overwrite`, no temporary left), the product workbook twice (`ExportFileExistsException`, then success), the support package and the data backup twice each. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportParametersTests.cs` (extended): the exports that rewrite the same path are now explicit replacements, and an export without asking onto the existing file is a Failed run recorded as such, with "üzerine yazılmadı", no directory in the message and the file intact; the backup writer test keeps its target outside the data directory (the service refuses a backup written into the folder it backs up).

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=ExportFileNames.cs (new), ReportRunner.cs, ReportsPanel.cs, RejectedRowsExport.cs, Catalog/CatalogExcel.cs, MainWindow.Excel.cs, AuditStore.cs, DiagnosticsPanel.cs, DataBackupService.cs, DataBackupPanel.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ExportFileNamesTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportParametersTests.cs, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=651 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 649 + 2 new); artifact SHA-256 3471d8ab65780b818aaffd1e8a53f8255cdc740bd7b4d27f7939277cdd4c049b (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the save dialogs keep Windows' own overwrite prompt as the confirmation (the application does not add a second one), and a path typed into a dialog is the operator's own name — it is not re-sanitized; the XML template export (`ExportSourceTemplate`) still writes its final path directly after the dialog (no temporary file); the Excel import profile and Etsy draft writers are not exports and were left as they are; a stale temporary file from a crashed export is not swept (#881); the "-2" suffix is chosen at suggestion time, so two operators choosing the same name in the same second on a shared folder still meet the dialog's prompt rather than a second suffix.

## #881 — Atomic file write completion (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-881-atomic-export-write off codex/issue-880-export-filenames (f6b4245)

Every export lands through one atomic seam, and a cancelled or failed export never leaves a half-written final file. `ExportFileNames.cs` (extended, the `ExportFiles` class): `TemporaryFor(path)` is the temporary next to the target — the same directory, so the same permissions as the final file and never a shared temp folder — named "<stem>.tmp-<12 hex><ext>" (the extension stays because ClosedXML insists on it); `Write(path, overwrite, cancellationToken, produce)` and `WriteAsync(...)` create the directory, sweep stale temporaries of the same target, check cancellation, let the producer fill the temporary, check cancellation again (a cancellation that arrived while the producer worked discards the complete temporary — the operator asked for no file), refuse a producer that wrote nothing (an `IOException`, never an empty final file), commit through `Commit` (an existing target is replaced only when asked, #880) and remove the temporary in every outcome; `SweepStale(path, olderThan = 30 min)` removes only this target's temporaries older than the window — what a run that crashed before its rename left behind — while a younger one (a run possibly in progress) and another target's are left alone. Every writer now hands its production to the seam instead of hand-rolling a temporary and a finally: `ReportRunner.ExportAsync` (CSV), `RejectedRowsExport.WriteCsv`, `CatalogExcel.ExportErrors` and `CatalogExcel.Export` (workbooks; the path-shape failures the removed preludes caught are now caught by the writers' outer `catch` and still become a sentence through the redaction), `SupportPackageService.Export` and `DataBackupService.Backup` (zips), the product XML export, and the XML template export, which used to write its final path directly and now goes through the same temporary-and-commit. Error messages keep passing through `AuditStore.Redact`.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ExportAtomicWriteTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): a complete write lands and its temporary lived in the target's directory under the target's stem with the target's extension and is gone afterwards; a producer that wrote half and threw "not enough space" leaves the target as it was and no temporary; a cancellation before the producer and one after it finished both leave the target untouched and no temporary; a producer that wrote nothing raises and no empty final file appears; a two-hour-old temporary of the same target is swept by the next write while a fresh one and another target's stay, and `SweepStale` with a zero window removes the other; an existing target without consent is refused with the temporary removed; the asynchronous form discards a temporary after a producer that awaited and then threw, and lands a complete one; the real rejected-rows writer with a cancelled token returns a cancelled result with neither a final file nor a temporary.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=ExportFileNames.cs, ReportRunner.cs, RejectedRowsExport.cs, Catalog/CatalogExcel.cs, AuditStore.cs, DataBackupService.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ExportAtomicWriteTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=652 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 651 + 1 new); artifact SHA-256 98c630f067ee2a590d845f179bb32361a90d690e0b874e044afa6e2dda3f5228 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: a crash between the producer's completion and the rename is only recovered by the next write of the same target (the stale sweep), not by a start-up sweep of every export folder (the application does not know where the operator exported); the temporary inherits the target directory's permissions and no tighter ACL is applied (a folder the operator chose is theirs); disk-full is simulated by a producer that throws, not by a real quota; the credential and settings stores keep their own temporary-and-move pattern (they are not exports); `File.Move` is the rename primitive (atomic on the same NTFS volume; a target on another volume is a copy-and-delete by the runtime).

## #882 — Source sample viewer limits (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-882-sample-viewer-limits off codex/issue-881-atomic-export-write (d210302)

The import mapping table's samples are bounded and their truncated state is explicit, and the sample item is read within limits instead of parsing the whole feed. `ImportSampleViewer.cs` (new): `Bound(raw, maxChars = 60)` is the specimen shown for one value — empty stays empty; binary-looking content (a NUL, or more than 5 % control, DEL or replacement characters in the first 512) becomes the fixed word "[ikili içerik gösterilmez]"; a raw body (JSON, XML, a stack) becomes the hidden-payload word; the rest passes the central redaction (an e-mail is "[pii-email]"), collapses its whitespace and, when longer than the limit, is cut at a grapheme boundary (`CutGraphemes` over `StringInfo` text elements — an emoji, a surrogate pair or a letter with its combining marks is kept or dropped whole) and carries the mark "… [kısaltıldı: N karakter]" with the source length — the truncated state is visible, never a silent ellipsis; the result names its kind (Empty / Text / Truncated / Binary / RawPayload) and the source length. `ReadFirstItem(xml, itemPath, limits)` streams the document with an `XmlReader` (DTD prohibited, no resolver) only as far as the first element whose name stack matches a plain item path and clones it within `SampleLimits` — at most 200 fields (attributes included), 8 levels, 4 096 characters per value read in chunks so a huge text node never materializes — and says whether anything was left out; a path that is not plain (a predicate, an attribute, a wildcard), a path that matches nothing, or a broken document gives no element and no exception. Wired: `ImportMappingTable.SafeSample` delegates to `Bound` (the mapping table's 60-character samples now carry the explicit mark); the XML page's sample item comes from `ReadFirstItem` (the former `XDocument.Parse(xml).XPathSelectElement(...)` — a full parse of the feed on the UI thread for one sample — remains only as the fallback for a non-plain item path) and the mapping summary appends "örnek kayıt kısaltıldı (alan veya uzunluk sınırı)" when the sample item was cut. Nothing here logs a value; the read logs only the number of field paths found, as before.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportSampleViewerTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): a two-million-character value cut to its first 60 characters plus the mark with the culture-formatted length; whitespace collapsed; empty and null; binary detection on NUL and on control characters, not on tabs and line breaks; the fixed binary word; an emoji run and a combining-mark run cut without splitting a pair, Turkish letters intact; an e-mail masked and a JSON body hidden; a 5 000-field row kept to 200 fields with its attribute and first field and without the last; a 100 000-character field and a 10 000-character CDATA kept to 4 096 each and the item marked cut; a depth limit leaving deep levels out while sibling paths stay walkable; a 200 000-item document whose first item is read in well under five seconds; a predicate path, a path matching nothing and a broken document giving no element; the mapping table's own sample carrying the mark. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportSampleViewerUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real XML page reading a feed whose first item carries a 300 000-character description ending in a marker, a binary-looking brand and an emoji title — the description sample starts with 60 d's and the mark and never the marker, the brand sample is the binary word, the title sample is intact, the SKU sample is plain, the mapping summary says the sample item was cut, and neither the samples, the reasons nor the operations log carry the marker. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportMappingTableTests.cs` (adjusted): a long description sample now ends with the explicit mark instead of a bare ellipsis.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=ImportSampleViewer.cs (new), ImportMappingTable.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportSampleViewerTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportSampleViewerUiTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportMappingTableTests.cs, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=654 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 652 + 2 new); artifact SHA-256 2f76a300777542a2d7ef2bef0a9b29f4cbc5a36d1066af17e8afc6d3b03f8d22 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the feed itself is still held in memory as one string (the reader streams over that string; reading a huge feed from disk in chunks is the reader's concern, not the sample viewer's); the sample cell has no separate "show more" affordance — the bounded text with its mark is all that is shown, by design; a non-plain item path (an XPath predicate) still falls back to the full parse for its sample; the Excel mapping dialog's samples (`ExcelMappingDialog`, #833) keep `ImportMappingTable.SafeSample`, so they gain the mark but not the streamed item (Excel previews are already row-bounded); the preview grid's cells (#825) are not samples and are unchanged.

## #883 — Correlation deep-link navigation (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-883-correlation-links off codex/issue-882-sample-viewer-limits (dd5ab0e)

One operation's events are one chain, reachable by a link and shown as safe summaries scoped to the stores this session may open. `AuditEvent.Correlation` (new column): `AuditStore.Append` stamps the id of the operation in flight (#879's activity registry — a guarded command, a shortcut, a measured view load) on every row written under it, keeps an explicit id when the caller passes one, and drops anything that is not an identifier (`CorrelationChain.SafeId`: letters, digits, dashes, 4–64); an audit store written before the column existed gains it in place (`PRAGMA table_info` then `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN Correlation TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''` and an index) with its rows keeping an empty correlation; `ListByCorrelation(id)` is the chain, oldest first. `CorrelationChain.cs` (new): `Compose(correlation, events, allowedStoreKeys, titleFor)` → `CorrelationChainView` — the visible events as `ChainEvent`s (time, module, action, outcome, a `Summarize`d detail: the central redaction, whitespace collapsed, a raw body replaced by the hidden-payload word, capped at 160; the store key; a `DrillTarget` to the order or the product the row names), the modules spanned, the outcome (hata when any row failed, uyarı when any warned, else tamam), the span, the number of events that belong to a store this session may not open (counted, never shown — store isolation), and a note for a chain nobody wrote or for hidden rows; `Visible`/`MayShow` apply the same store rule to raw rows; `Headline` is the one-line "N olay · modules · span · outcome"; `Link(id, store)` is `monobridge://correlation/<id>[?store=…]` and `WorkspaceLinks` knows the kind "correlation" → route "diagnostics", so the link travels the same trail, refusal (a store this session may not open is refused before any screen changes) and reveal as every workspace link (#813). `DiagnosticsPanel.Create(directory, navigate, exposeReveal, allowedStoreKeys, openLink)`: a "Korelasyon" box with "Zinciri aç", "Seçili kaydın zinciri" (also a double-click on a row) and "Listeye dön", a summary line (tag `correlation-summary`) with the headline and the hidden-rows note, entity buttons (tag `correlation-entity`) that open the order or product through the shell's `OpenWorkspaceLink`, a "Korelasyon" column in the audit grid, and the exposed `Reveal(correlation)` the shell's `RevealEntity` calls for the "correlation" kind (`diagnosticsReveal`, like the orders reveal). Nothing here shows a log line: the grid shows the audit rows the session may see, the summary the sanitized detail.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CorrelationChainTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): an old audit.db created without the column is opened, migrated and its row read with an empty correlation; rows written inside an activity carry its id, a row outside none, an explicit id is kept and a non-identifier dropped; the chain of four rows comes back in time order and an unknown or hostile id has none; the chain scoped to the etsy store — three events across import, job and order, "hata" from the failed one, the e-mail masked, the JSON body hidden, a product link and an order link with the store and the orders route, the ebay row counted as hidden with its note, a headline naming the count and the outcome, summaries capped; a wrong store hiding all four with the count; no allowed list meaning every store; a missing and an invalid correlation as empty views with their notes; the link's exact form, its parse to the diagnostics route with the store, a hostile id refused, a bad id refused by `Link`; a second store instance (a restart) reading the same chain. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CorrelationChainUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real main window with an enabled etsy connection and five seeded rows — a link scoped to etsy opens the audit centre on the chain's three rows with a summary naming "3 olay" and "uyarı" and no e-mail, the box filled, an order button offered; a link scoped to ebay (not enabled) is refused with the dashboard still showing; a missing correlation opens the centre with the note and an empty grid; by hand, the ebay chain shows "1 kayıt" hidden instead of its row, the store-less chain its one event, and "Listeye dön" restores the full list. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/WorkspaceLinkTests.cs` (adjusted): the set of link kinds now includes "correlation".

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=CorrelationChain.cs (new), AuditStore.cs, WorkspaceLinks.cs, DiagnosticsPanel.cs, MainWindow.Navigation.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CorrelationChainTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CorrelationChainUiTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/WorkspaceLinkTests.cs, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=656 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 654 + 2 new); artifact SHA-256 1c1cf325980d1fe5ebce7c312b5308720fa5051ec694d816e744bf183a1ee42d (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the XML run records (`XmlRunStore`) and the sync jobs (`SyncStore`) carry no correlation of their own — they appear in a chain only through the audit rows their operations write, so a job that wrote no audit row is not in any chain; a chain does not link to the source the import read (audit rows carry no source id); rows written outside any activity (a timer tick, a background scan) have no correlation and are found only by the plain audit search; the correlation column is not searched by the audit search box (the "Seçili kaydın zinciri" button and the box are the way in); the error banner (#816) does not yet offer "open this chain" for the failure it shows; the copy-to-clipboard summary is #884.

## #884 — Safe copy summary (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-884-safe-copy-summary off codex/issue-883-correlation-links (1791b04)

A failure or a recorded run becomes a support text in one click, redacted by the central redactor, never a raw exception or payload. `SupportSummary.cs` (new): `Compose(error, context)` writes the header, the version, the moment (UTC), the screen and source the surface knows, the operation's correlation id, the failure's class and one-line summary (`ErrorBanner.Describe`: "İşlem yapılamadı (kalıcı)" or "Geçici hata (yeniden denenebilir)", the busy-database diagnosis for a locked store), the exception chain as type names with sanitized messages — an aggregate unwrapped, five levels then "… (daha derin iç hatalar kısaltıldı)", never a stack frame — and the recent audit events (ten at most) as safe summaries; `ComposeFromAudit(record, related, context)` does the same for an audit row (module/action/outcome/time, store, order, product, the sanitized detail, its chain); `Safe` replaces a raw body, redacts, collapses whitespace and caps a line at 300; and every finished line passes `AuditStore.Redact` last, so nothing composed can slip past the central redaction. `SafeClipboard.TryCopy(text)` puts the text on the clipboard through a replaceable setter and answers false — never an exception — when the clipboard is unavailable (a COM or interop failure, no STA, an access refusal). Surfaces: the error banner (#816) gained the action "Özeti kopyala" (automation name "Destek özetini kopyala") whenever its surface can compose a summary — `ErrorSurface` now keeps the exception it shows and the correlation of the operation that was running — the button says "Kopyalandı ✓" on success and, when the clipboard is unavailable, a read-only text box (tag `support-summary-fallback`) appears inside the banner holding the note and the text for a manual copy; the diagnostics page gained "Son hatanın özetini kopyala" (the last recorded failure with its chain, or the ten most recent rows when it has no correlation) and "Seçili kaydın özetini kopyala" (the selected audit row with its chain), the status line says "Destek özeti panoya kopyalandı; kişisel veri ve gizli değerler maskelendi." or "Pano kullanılamadı; özet aşağıda, elle kopyalayın." with the same read-only fallback box shown on the page.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SupportSummaryTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): a three-level nested error with an e-mail, a user path and a token in its messages — the context lines, the class, the three types, "[user]" and no e-mail, token or stack frame; a seven-level chain cut after the fifth with the note; a connector failure with a query secret — retryable, the secret gone; a locked database — the busy diagnosis and the type; a raw JSON message hidden; an aggregate unwrapped; a recorded failure with its chain — the row, store, order, correlation, the related event, a phone masked; ten recent events at most; a field capped; the clipboard recording on success and reporting a COM failure and an STA failure without throwing. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SupportSummaryUiTests.cs` (new, 1): a real banner shown inside a running operation — the copy action puts the redacted summary (header, class, type, correlation, screen; no e-mail, no secret, no stack frame) on the recording clipboard and says so on the button, then with the clipboard failing shows the text read-only inside the banner; the diagnostics page over a seeded failed order row and its import row — "Son hatanın özetini kopyala" copies the row with its chain and no phone or secret and reports it in the status line, "Seçili kaydın özetini kopyala" copies the selected row, and with the clipboard failing the page shows the text in its fallback box with the note. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ErrorBannerControlTests.cs` (adjusted): the banner's action list now carries the copy action before "Kapat".

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=SupportSummary.cs (new), ErrorBanner.cs, DiagnosticsPanel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SupportSummaryTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SupportSummaryUiTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ErrorBannerControlTests.cs, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=658 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 656 + 2 new); artifact SHA-256 9441aa9709e65da1eee61bf68473d918392d25c122764386c330d829838e7a3d (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the report run view (#848) keeps its "Tanılamaya git" button and does not copy its own diagnostics text (the diagnostics page's "last failure" covers a failed run through its audit row); toasts (#814) have no copy action; the summary carries the exception types and messages, not the stack (by design — a support engineer asks for the crash log, which the global handler already writes sanitized to crash.log); the clipboard setter is process-wide state the tests replace and restore; the "Kopyalandı ✓" confirmation on the banner button does not revert on its own.

## #885 — Global loading overlay deadlock guard (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-885-busy-overlay off codex/issue-884-safe-copy-summary (640e5a8)

The shell's loading state is reference-counted per owner and its overlay can only be open while an owner is in. Before this the shell signalled "busy" by disabling the whole tab control in two places (`RunAsync` and the scheduled tick), each re-enabling it in its own finally — one path forgetting the other, or a nested operation, would have left the shell dead. `BusyState.cs` (new): `Enter(owner, label, cancel?)` returns a token; the same owner may nest (`Depth`), a second dispose of a token is a no-op, an exception or a cancellation in the work leaves through the same disposal (`RunAsync(owner, label, work, cancel?)` wraps it), `ReleaseAll(owner)` drops an owner outright — for a screen left while its load is in flight — and `ReleaseEverything()` is the terminal safety net; `Changed` fires on every transition; `Owners`, `Headline` (the one label, or "N işlem sürüyor · <latest>"), `CanCancel`; `CancelAll()` runs only the owners' cancel actions, once each, and nothing else — an overlay action can never reach a confirmation-gated command; keys are identifiers, labels pass the central redaction and are capped. `BusyOverlay.Create(state)` is the veil over the workspace (tag `busy-overlay`, z-index under the toasts): the headline, an indeterminate bar, and one button — "İptal", shown only while an owner can be cancelled — plus Escape doing the same; it renders from the state's `Changed` alone, so when the last owner leaves it closes, certainly. Wired: the shell layer grid is named `ShellLayers` and the overlay is added over it at construction; `RunAsync` enters "shell" with a Turkish label for the operation (`BusyLabel`: import, preview, XML read, Excel apply/preview, reachability check) and a cancel that cancels the import stage token, and the scheduled tick enters "scheduler" ("Zamanlanmış kontrol sürüyor…", not cancellable); neither disables the tab control any more; the single-flight gate ("Önceki işlem sürüyor.") is unchanged.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/BusyStateTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): nested entries of one owner busy until the last leaves, a second dispose a no-op, never negative; two owners counted in the headline; an exception and a cancellation both leaving; cancel running the cancel action once and nothing else, an owner without one not cancellable; dropping an owner and releasing everything; a hundred interleaved scopes ending idle after double disposes; keys and labels safe. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/BusyOverlayUiTests.cs` (new, 1): a plain window — the overlay closed, open with the redacted headline, no cancel for an owner that cannot be stopped, exactly one action, cancel visible for a cancellable owner and running its action once while the button beneath is never reached, no second cancel after one, still open while another owner is in, closed when the last leaves, Escape cancelling; the real shell — a guarded operation opens the overlay, a second operation is refused without touching it, navigation while busy leaves it, completion, an exception and a cancellation each close it.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=BusyState.cs (new), MainWindow.xaml, MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/BusyStateTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/BusyOverlayUiTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=660 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 658 + 2 new); artifact SHA-256 08e3d695cde5454b39f54e51e02448e44217a65d3282150945165477f0422555 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the panels' own busy affordances (the dashboard refresh, the orders refresh, the report run, the bulk drawer — `CommandState.Busy` on their buttons) stay local and do not enter the shell's state, so a panel-level load shows no global overlay (by design: the overlay is for operations that own the whole shell); the overlay's cancel reaches only the import stage token for a shell operation — an Excel apply or a reachability check has no cancel of its own here; there is no timeout that force-closes an overlay whose owner never returns (the owner's disposal is the guarantee; a hung owner is the freeze watchdog's #879 concern); the overlay does not trap keyboard focus inside itself (it takes focus when shown and blocks pointer input beneath).

## #886 — Empty-state illustration-free standard (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-886-empty-state-standard off codex/issue-885-busy-overlay (8223f22)

Every workspace's empty state has one shape and no image: a text glyph, a title, the reason, and at most one action this build can perform — with a filtered empty told apart from a true empty and from a hidden one. `EmptyState.cs` (new): `EmptyStateKind` TrueEmpty / FilteredEmpty / Hidden with a glyph per kind (▢ ⌕ ⊘ — text, never a picture); `EmptyStateView(Kind, Glyph, Title, Reason, ActionLabel, Route, ActionKey)` with `HasAction`; `Compose(kind, title, reason, actionLabel, route, routeExists)` offers a route action only when the shell registers that route (a missing screen drops the button and keeps the diagnosis — "unsupported action gösterme"), `Local(...)` offers the workspace's own action by key (`clear-filter`, `new-source`); titles and reasons pass the central redaction and are capped. The standard states: `Products(filtered)` — "Henüz ürün yok" → XML kaynaklarını aç, or "Bu aramaya uyan ürün yok" → Aramayı ve filtreleri temizle; `Orders(filtered)` — "Henüz sipariş yok" → Mağaza bağlantılarını aç, or "Bu filtreye uyan sipariş yok" → Filtreleri temizle; `Sources()` — "Henüz XML kaynağı yok" → Yeni kaynak; `Preview(filtered)` — "Önizleme yok" / "Filtreye uyan önizleme satırı yok"; `Reports(view, searching)` — none defined / hidden by store scope → Mağaza bağlantılarını aç / "Aramaya uyan rapor yok" → Aramayı temizle; `Dashboard(ladder)` — the #811 ladder in the shared shape (its FILTERED_EMPTY step is the filtered kind); `Matrix(filtered, reason)`. `EmptyStatePanel.Render(host, state, navigate, local)` builds the panel (tags `empty-state`, `-title`, `-reason`, `-action`; the glyph as a status-sized run on the title's baseline, the reason in the hint role, the action a real focusable button at the hit-target minimum; wrapping text, a 640 DIP maximum width, the severity surface for the kind, an automation name of title and reason) and collapses the host when there is nothing to say. Wired: the product list (a host above the grid; rendered on every page show; filtered = a search text or a non-default `CatalogFilter` — `CatalogFilter.IsDefault` is new — and the clear action resets both and refreshes), the order list (a host above the split; rendered after every filter pass; filtered = any search, state, marketplace, shop, stock or urgency filter; the clear action resets them all), the XML source list (a host under the list; the new-source action is the page's own add) and the XML preview grid (a host above the grid, rendered from the import stepper refresh and the preview filter), the report catalogue (the former plain text becomes the shared panel; `ReportsPanel.Create` takes `routeExists`), the dashboard (`ShowEmptyState` renders the ladder through the shared panel). `OrdersPanel.Create` and `ReportsPanel.Create` take the shell's `navigate` and `routeExists`.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/EmptyStateTests.cs` (new, 1, red first): a route this build lacks drops the action, a route check absent drops it too, a local action carries its key; each workspace state's kind, title and action (products, orders with and without a connections screen, sources, preview, the three report cases, the dashboard ladder in both kinds, the matrix); a capped title and a masked reason; in a real window — the tags, no image, a focusable action at the hit-target minimum that navigates or runs the local action, no button for an unsupported action, a collapsed host for nothing, and a long Turkish title and reason that wrap at 300 DIP with nothing clipped by the overflow audit. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/EmptyStateUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real main window over an empty directory — the dashboard's ladder in the shared shape, the product pool's true empty whose action opens the XML screen, the source list's true empty with "Yeni kaynak", the order list's true empty with "Mağaza bağlantılarını aç", the report catalogue's filtered empty whose action clears the search and brings the cards back, and — after two products are imported — no empty state, then a search that matches nothing showing the filtered empty whose action clears the search and brings the two rows back. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportsPanelUiTests.cs` (adjusted): the report empty state is the shared panel; its visibility and title are read from it.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=EmptyState.cs (new), Catalog/CatalogFilter.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, MainWindow.Filters.cs, MainWindow.Navigation.cs, OrdersPanel.cs, ReportsPanel.cs, DashboardPanel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/EmptyStateTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/EmptyStateUiTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportsPanelUiTests.cs, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=662 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 660 + 2 new); artifact SHA-256 997da954a7777777b0783d14137490ee3ecfca4aaa45a039cb2604c3970d2b97 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the channel matrix keeps its own empty text (`channel-matrix-empty`, #844) and legend-chip reset — not routed through the shared panel; the XML preview's filtered empty offers no clear action of its own (the filter bar is the place; the reason says so); the report result surface (#850) keeps its own six-kind states and CTAs; the settings search's "Bu aramaya uyan ayar yok" line and the notification centre's "Açık uyarı yok" line are single sentences and stay so; DPI is covered by DIP-only measures and the overflow audit at one width, not a DPI matrix (#887).

## #887 — WPF visual-state regression harness (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-887-visual-state-harness off codex/issue-886-empty-state-standard (f6a1a88)

Semantic layout and state assertions instead of pixel locks. `VisualStateAudit.cs` (new): `VisualStateSnapshot(Route, Width, Scale, EmptyStates, Errors, Busy, GridRows, Actions, Overflow)` with `StateOnly` (the room-independent part) and `Semantic` (with the cut count); `VisualStateAudit.Snapshot(root, route, width, scale)` reads a screen from its tree — the shared empty-state titles (#886), the error-banner titles (#816), the shell's busy overlay (#885; read from the window, since it is a shell layer above every workspace), the visible grids' row counts, the offered actions (visible enabled buttons outside a control template — a grid's select-all corner or a date picker's drop button is chrome; data-template buttons count) and the `OverflowAudit` cut count (#867); `IsSettling(root)` (a visible command disabled with a Busy reason, #871); `VisibleTexts(root)` (every visible TextBlock through TextRange, editable boxes excluded as the user's own content); `PersonalDataOnScreen(root)` (the texts the central `AuditStore.Redact` would change — e-mail, phone, bearer value, key=value credential, user-profile path — with the words secret/token as vocabulary neutralised unless used as a key); `ApplyDpiScale(window, scale, baseWidth, baseHeight)` = a `ScaleTransform` LayoutTransform on the window's content with the client area grown in step (the frame is measured through `GetClientRect` and never scaled), so the content keeps exactly the DIP room it has at 1.0 in a base-size window, and `HasRoom` says whether the desktop honoured the size. The desktop caps every top-level window at the virtual screen through WM_GETMINMAXINFO and WPF's `Window` records that cap as its own limit (its `GetWindowMinMax` takes the smaller of MaxWidth and the recorded track maximum, and its Measure/Arrange clamp to it — so neither `MaxWidth` nor a handled hook can grow a window past the screen); the audit lifts the cap per window with an `HwndSource` hook that enlarges `ptMaxTrackSize`/`ptMaxSize` in place without marking the message handled — hooks run newest first, so WPF's own handler then records the enlarged limits as the desktop's and reports them back. `DataBackupPanel.cs`: the "Yerel veri:" line showed the data directory with the Windows username in it — the harness's first real finding — and now goes through `AuditStore.Redact` (username segment masked, the rest of the path kept).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/VisualStateHarnessTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): (1) a fixture window — the snapshot reads the empty-state title, the grid's rows, the busy flag, the actions (template chrome excluded), personal data through the same redaction (an e-mail line flagged, an order line not), a 1.5 scale keeps the content's DIP room (`ActualWidth` unchanged while the window grew) and an invalid scale throws; (2) the real main window over two temp roots — empty root: dashboard, products, xml, orders, listing-matrix and settings each at 1440 and at MinWidth (1150) with zero cut elements and no personal data, then the DPI matrix 1.0 / 1.25 / 1.5 / 2.0 with the state and the cut count identical at every scale, the products/orders/xml true-empty titles and the dashboard ladder asserted, and every other registered route (29) swept once for personal data; populated root (a synthetic fixture: three products with long Turkish names, two sources — one whose file is gone —, one order, one sync job, one failed run, one failed audit row, one connection; no live call): the grids carry 3 / 1 rows, no empty state, no error, nothing busy, same width and DPI checks; loading: a gated `RunAsync` shows the busy overlay at every scale and it is gone when the operation ends; error (import): reading the missing file faults `InspectAsync`, the Download stage is Failed and the page is the same at every scale; error (dashboard): the notification ledger set read-only makes "Durumu yenile" fail into exactly one error banner at every scale, the banner never quotes the data directory, and the next refresh clears it. ~22 s locally; every scale honoured on this machine.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=VisualStateAudit.cs (new), DataBackupPanel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/VisualStateHarnessTests.cs (new); TEST_RESULT=664 passed, 0 failed (full Release suite; last green run 662, +2); artifact: self-contained win-x64 Release publish, SHA-256 a80275d57649f742b695a95bc2a56edd78e7c15a645221aa51766ddd350287f5 (publish/ not committed).

Not done: the snapshot reads six state facets (empty states, banners, busy, grid rows, actions, cut count) — texts, colours and positions are not compared, by design; the DPI matrix scales the content with a layout transform and grows the window in step, which proves DIP invariance under scaling, not a real per-monitor DPI change (no WM_DPICHANGED, and WPF's text path at a real DPI is not exercised); the size-cap lift stays in force for a window until it closes; the loading state is proven on the shell's busy overlay only (per-panel Busy-disabled commands are read for settling, not driven); the error state is driven on the import page and the dashboard only; the channel matrix's own empty text (#844) is not a shared empty state, so its empty fixture reads as rows only; inner tabs of a route (the channel pages' Bağlantı tab, the XML page's later steps) are not opened in the personal-data sweep; the sweep treats the words secret/token as vocabulary (seven help lines and the readiness check id use them), so only a key=value use of them is probed.

## #888 — WPF accessibility regression suite (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-888-accessibility-suite off codex/issue-887-visual-state-harness (107c2a9)

What a keyboard or screen-reader user meets on a screen, asked of the tree and asserted on the main screens. `AccessibilityAudit.cs` (new): a keyboard unit is an input or command (`ButtonBase`, `TextBoxBase`, `PasswordBox`, `ComboBox`, `DatePicker`, `Slider`), a code-built surface made a keyboard stop (#861), or a container entered as one stop (`DataGrid`, `ListBox`, `TabControl`, `TreeView`); chrome inside a control's template is the control's own business. `AccessibleName(element)` = the automation name, the labelling element's text, the content's text or a text tooltip; `Names(root)` → `Unnamed` for an input or surface with none; `FocusVisuals(root)` → `NoFocusVisual` for a focusable input whose ring was switched off; `DisabledReasons(root)` → `DisabledWithoutReason` for a disabled input with no #871 reason on it or an ancestor and no tooltip that shows while disabled; `Walk(root)` moves keyboard focus with `TraversalRequest(Next)` from the first enabled unit through the whole window until the cycle comes back (`KeyboardWalk(Stops, Closed)`, limit 800); `Reachability(root, walk)` → `FocusTrap` when the cycle never closed and `Unreachable` for an enabled unit the walk touched neither itself nor through a descendant (items inside a grid or list are arrow-key territory; an empty grid or list is skipped by Tab by WPF's own design and is not required); `Audit(root, walk?)` = all four; findings name their owner (type, name/tag/announced name/content, a grid's first headers and row count) and the text that precedes it, sanitized. `SeverityStyle.IsHighContrast` gained an internal `HighContrastOverride` so a test can pin the desktop's high-contrast state. Findings fixed at their source: `FormField.Labelled(label, control)` ties a text block to the control it labels (LabeledBy + Name) and is now used by the five raw `AddLabel` helpers (Amazon, Fruugo, Hepsiburada, Trendyol, MarketplaceConnections), by `MainWindow.Label` (the product editor's expiry date picker, the XML source form's Basic-Auth user and password, the decimal separator) and by `ChannelProductsPanel.Field` (nine plan fields; the read-only product card is named); the order list's status, marketplace, store and stock filters, its saved-view combo and the detail's shipment and shipping-state combos are named, as are the XML page's and the sync centre's saved-view combos and the mapping grid's XML-path selector.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/AccessibilityRegressionTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): (1) a fixture window with the app's token styles — a clean form (named button, named box, check box, named combo, a disabled button with a #871 reason, an icon button with a spoken name, a grid) has no finding; a dirty form yields exactly Unnamed (a button whose content is a shape), Unreachable (a box that is not a tab stop), DisabledWithoutReason (a disabled button with nothing to say) and NoFocusVisual (a focusable surface with the ring removed); a reason or a tooltip shown while disabled clears the disabled finding; the walk closes on its start and visits the form in document order; the name resolves from the automation name, the label, the content and the tooltip; (2) the real main window over a seeded root (two products, a source, an order): products with a product selected, orders, xml with the source selected, the Trendyol page's product plans and its connection form, and settings — every screen audited with zero findings and more than five keyboard stops each; the shortcut reference (F1) and a destructive `DialogShell` confirmation audited as windows of their own, the confirmation's Tab cycle being exactly its two buttons with the safe button as the default; then, with the desktop's high-contrast state pinned, a fresh main window's product empty state takes the system highlight and window colours, `FocusStyles.Create()` draws the ring in the system's window-text and window brushes, `ContrastAudit.Audit(true)` passes every non-system pair, and the products screen audits clean under high contrast.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=AccessibilityAudit.cs (new), SeverityStyle.cs, FormField.cs, AmazonPanel.cs, FruugoPanel.cs, HepsiburadaPanel.cs, TrendyolPanel.cs, MarketplaceConnectionsPanel.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, MainWindow.Sync.cs, ChannelProductsPanel.cs, OrdersPanel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/AccessibilityRegressionTests.cs (new); TEST_RESULT=666 passed, 0 failed (full Release suite; last green run 664, +2); artifact: self-contained win-x64 Release publish, SHA-256 4749013bfd27b6462647585ae376590955f7f7f736a67b34d15ac2bdfa99e843 (publish/ not committed).

Not done: the audit covers the five screens the issue names plus two dialogs, not every registered route (the other 30 screens and the channel pages other than Trendyol are not asserted, though their label helpers were fixed alongside); containers (grids, lists, tab strips) are required to be reachable but not to carry a name of their own; the Tab walk asserts the cycle and coverage, not that the order matches the visual reading order; the focus-visual check asks whether a ring style exists (the ring actually drawing under keyboard input is #861's own test); high contrast is pinned through the code-built owners' seam — the token file's XAML DataTrigger still reads the real desktop, so XAML-styled controls are not exercised in high contrast; the products page's filter bar sits in a collapsed expander and its combos were not audited (collapsed content is not in the tree).

## #889 — Scoped navigation history (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-889-scoped-history off codex/issue-888-accessibility-suite (9660229)

The drill-through trail (#810, #813) now keeps its scope in both directions and across a restart. `NavigationDrillThrough.cs`: `DrillTarget` gains `Revision` (the record's version stamp as it was when the step was taken); `DrillThroughStack` keeps a forward branch — `Back` leaves the step it left in front, `Forward(entityAlive, revisionOf)` walks it with the same checks (`CanGoForward`), a new `Open` or a store switch drops the branch, the trail is capped at 50 steps; `Back`/`Forward` land through one path: a record that is gone degrades to its screen (as before, now for both directions), and a record whose revision changed meanwhile is landed on with `RevisionChanged` and the note "… kayıt bu arada değişti; güncel hali gösteriliyor" while the step remembers the new revision; `SafeLabel(target)` makes every crumb PII-safe — a label the central redaction would change falls back to the screen's title, and a label is capped at 60; `Snapshot()`/`Restore(saved, routeExists, allowedStoreKeys, entityAlive)` re-validate a saved trail under this session's rules: a root store this session does not offer leaves the whole trail behind, a screen this build lacks or a step that fails the live store check cuts the trail there, a record that is gone keeps its screen and drops the record, the forward branch goes through the same gate, and saved labels are re-sanitized on the way in; `DrillHistoryCodec` (`PreferenceKey "shell:trail"`, `Serialize`, `TryDeserialize`) is strict — route keys by shape, every string bounded at 200 characters and free of control characters, at most 50 steps, labels that fail the redaction dropped — so a tampered or stale preference cannot put anything into the shell. Shell wiring: `PreferenceSchema` family `shell:trail`; `MainWindow.Navigation.cs` restores the trail before the last-route navigation (`RestoreTrail()` logs every notice; a trail with depth decides the opening screen and its current record is revealed), stamps the revision on every drill and workspace link (`Stamped` via `DrillEntityRevision`: a product's `UpdatedUtc`, an order's `UpdatedAt`, a source's `MappingRevision`), lands Back and Forward through one `Land` (notice, route, reveal), and `RefreshBackButton` explains both buttons (#871); `MainWindow.xaml` gains `ForwardButton` ("İleri ›") right of Back; `MainWindow.xaml.cs` gives it the inline icon style and the gesture tooltip; the trail is saved with every navigation (`SaveTrail` beside the last-route write in `SelectRoute` and on a store switch), not on close, so a preference reset is not undone by closing the window and a crash keeps the last step taken; `KeyboardShortcuts` gains `forward` = Alt+→ and `HandleShortcut` walks it.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NavigationHistoryTests.cs` (new, 5; they name members that did not exist before): forward walks the branch Back left and a new step drops it; a changed record is noticed on Back and on Forward with the new revision kept, an empty or unreadable revision never claims a change; a forward step whose record vanished degrades to its screen; crumbs never carry personal data (an e-mail label becomes the title, a 200-character label is capped, the saved payload carries neither) ; the history round-trips through the codec and `Restore` re-validates stores (the trail is left behind), screens (cut), records (degraded), forward included, and refuses empty, malformed, path-shaped, oversized and over-long payloads while a saved label with personal data comes back empty. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NavigationHistoryWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): in the real window — drill to a product, Back enables "İleri ›" (tooltip names Alt+→), Alt+→ walks forward and selects the product again and is not consumed when nothing is in front, a store switch drops the branch; restart: the trail comes back, opens on the product screen with the crumb and the selection; restart after deleting the product: the screen stays and the crumb stops claiming the record; restart with a saved trail naming a store this session does not offer: nothing comes back.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=NavigationDrillThrough.cs, PreferenceSchema.cs, KeyboardShortcuts.cs, MainWindow.UX.cs, MainWindow.xaml, MainWindow.xaml.cs, MainWindow.Navigation.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NavigationHistoryTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/NavigationHistoryWindowTests.cs (new); TEST_RESULT=672 passed, 0 failed (full Release suite; last green run 666, +6); artifact: self-contained win-x64 Release publish, SHA-256 514839388dec9efd38dcddbe3db1ec94497fe4a331748dedc497ea2696c8691a (publish/ not committed).

Not done: the revision is a stamp read at step time (a product's update time, an order's update time, a source's mapping revision) — a correlation id or a listing revision is not part of it, and a kind without a stamp never reports a change; the dashboard's own store filter is persisted separately (`filter:dashboard-store`) and the restored trail's root store is not reconciled with it beyond the shell's scope check; the crumb text stays elided to four visible steps; a deep link followed while a trail is restored is stamped like any other step, but a link that arrives before the window finished restoring is not queued.

## #890 — Cancellation outcome messaging (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-890-cancellation-outcome off codex/issue-889-scoped-history (20b9540)

A cancel click is a request, not an outcome. `CancellationOutcome.cs` (new): `CancellationPhase` NotRequested / Requested / StoppingAtSafePoint / Cancelled / CompletedBeforeCancel; `CancellationFacts(RequestedUtc, Acknowledged, Ended, EndedUtc, StageCancellable, StageLabel)` — what a surface knows for certain (the operator's request, the pipeline's acknowledgement, whether and when the operation reached its end, whether the stage under way can be interrupted); `CancellationOutcome.Describe(facts, stayedSafe)` → `CancellationVerdict(Phase, Headline, Detail)` with `Line`, `IsTerminal`, `Level`: "İptal istendi · Sürmekte olan aşama bir sonraki kontrolde duracak." while the request waits, "Güvenli noktada duruyor · <Aşama> aşaması bölünemez; bittiğinde iş durur." while a stage that cannot be interrupted finishes its unit, "İptal edildi · <what stayed safe>" once the pipeline acknowledged (the surface's own sentence, redacted), "Tamamlanmıştı · …" when the job had ended before the request ("istek geldiğinde iş bitmişti") or in spite of it ("son güvenli noktadan sonra geldi") — an acknowledgement outranks an end stamp; `ForSyncJob(before, cancelled)` for the sync queue: a pending job stops before any request leaves, a running one finishes the request already on the wire and writes its own result, an ended one stays what it was. `ImportProgressState` and `ReportRunProgressState` keep the request apart from the acknowledgement: `CancelRequestedUtc`, `RequestCancel(now)`, `BeginOperation()` (a new operation starts with no request pending), `CancelAcknowledged` (a stage reported cancelled by the pipeline), the pipeline's own policy `IsCancellable(stage)` (the import's read is one parse that runs to its end — every other stage checks the token as it goes and the apply is one transaction that rolls back; the report's generation is one unit, the query and the export check row by row), and `CancellationFacts(now)` (ended = nothing runs and a stage finished or failed; the end stamp is the latest finished stage's end); `Retry()` clears the request. Surfaces: the import page's cancel buttons (read and preview/apply) now call `RequestCancel` and cancel the token — they no longer rewrite the running stage as cancelled — and `RenderImportProgress` shows the verdict in `importCancelOutcome` (Tag `cancel-outcome`, severity-coloured, "Havuz değişmedi; yazılan satır yok." as what stays safe) and holds the cancel button with a Busy reason ("İptal istendi; yanıt bekleniyor.") until the verdict is terminal; the report run view gets a `cancelOutcome` line beside its headline ("Dosya yazılmadı; sonuç değişmedi."), its cancel click records the request and reports the verdict as the status, and `BeginRun` starts a new operation; the sync centre gains "Seçili işi iptal et" (Tag `sync-cancel`) whose status line is `ForSyncJob` over the store's answer — the store's own `Cancel` flips only pending or running rows, so a finished job keeps its result.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CancellationOutcomeTests.cs` (new, 3; they name members that did not exist before): the four words follow the state — not requested, requested (early/mid), safe point (a non-cancellable stage), cancelled with the guarantee, late (ended before the request), the race (ended after the request without an acknowledgement), acknowledgement over end stamp, and no credential or address survives the guarantee sentence; the sync verdicts for pending, running, succeeded, failed, already-cancelled and unchanged rows; the import and report states keep the request apart from the acknowledgement (the click does not rewrite the stage, the read/generation are the non-cancellable stages, the acknowledgement arrives with the pipeline's event, retry clears the request, every stage done after the request is the race). `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CancellationOutcomeUiTests.cs` (new, 3): the import page on the real pipeline — a click after a local read finished says "Tamamlanmıştı … bitmişti"; a request during the download says "İptal istendi" and disables the button with the waiting reason while the stage stays running; the parse under way says "Güvenli noktada duruyor · Oku aşaması bölünemez"; the pipeline's cancelled event says "İptal edildi · Havuz değişmedi" and shows retry; retry hides the line; the report run view with an injected query that honours the token — the click alone says "İptal istendi" in the status and the outcome line, the answer makes it "İptal edildi · Dosya yazılmadı" with the query stage stopped — and with a query that ignores the token and finishes — "İptal istendi" until it ends, then "Tamamlanmıştı · son güvenli noktadan sonra" with the result standing and nothing to retry; the sync centre — a queued job is cancelled ("istek gitmedi", the row is Cancelled), a finished job says "Tamamlanmıştı … bitmişti" and stays Succeeded.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=CancellationOutcome.cs (new), ImportProgress.cs, ReportRunProgress.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, ReportParameterPanel.cs, MainWindow.Sync.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CancellationOutcomeTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CancellationOutcomeUiTests.cs (new); TEST_RESULT=678 passed, 0 failed (full Release suite; last green run 672, +6); artifact: self-contained win-x64 Release publish, SHA-256 79d64298b48dc76b693b077247dce285018aa91ff0ca6101d4e8a0b19b5e7b2f (publish/ not committed).

Not done: the import's "safe point" and "requested" phases are proven by driving the real progress state through the real cancel handler and renderer, not by holding the real pipeline mid-parse (a local feed parses in milliseconds; the race case does run the real pipeline); a running sync job has no token on the wire — the store marks it cancelled and the worker's own result overwrites that when the request completes, which the words say but no test drives a live worker; the bulk matrix drawer keeps its own cancel sentence (#845) and the busy overlay's cancel (#885) still only cancels the token; the report run view's `EndRun` inference (a run that returned with a stage still running is marked cancelled) is unchanged; the ended stamp is the latest finished stage's end, so a run whose last stage is still pending on purpose (a query without an export) reads as ended — which is what the operator sees.

## #891 — End-to-end operator design acceptance (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-891-operator-acceptance off codex/issue-890-cancellation-outcome (47e21d9)

One operator flow through the real window, scored by the shared audits, plus the same flow as a written acceptance script for the packaged app. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OperatorAcceptanceTests.cs` (new, 1): over a synthetic multi-store fixture (`Seed`: two enabled stores etsy/S1 and trendyol/T1, a feed on disk whose titles include a 100+-character Turkish name, three products, a local channel plan, an order whose customer record carries a synthetic name, e-mail, phone and address that must stay masked, one queued sync job — nothing live) the flow runs dashboard → product list and workspace → XML read and import preview → channel matrix → order workspace → report → settings. Step 0 verifies the design tokens resolve, the window's base font and size are the tokens and the shortcut catalogue has no conflicts. Step 1 settles the board, refuses a deep link naming a store this session does not offer without moving, and drills to the product with the crumb naming its SKU. Step 2 finds the drilled product selected, then drives the real "Ürünü sil" confirmation through the modal pump: the safe button is the default, "Sil" is not, the dialog's inputs are named, cancelling keeps the product. Step 3 selects the source and runs the real read and preview (`InspectAsync`, `PreviewAsync`) so the preview grid carries the feed's rows; the apply is never clicked. Step 4 lists the matrix. Step 5 reveals the order through the orders workspace and asserts the customer's synthetic name and address never appear on screen. Step 6 opens the orders report's setup, runs the query on the real store until the result is on screen and asserts no CSV was written. Step 7 opens settings. Every step is scored by `Score()`: no cut element at 1440 and at the minimum width, no text the central redaction would change, the same semantic state at 100 / 150 / 200 % (the #887 DPI matrix), and a clean accessibility audit (#888: names, focus visuals, disabled reasons, Tab reachability). Then keyboard only: the Tab cycle from the search box comes back to its start with the keyboard as the last input device, Ctrl+1 opens the board, Ctrl+K reaches the search, an unbound key is not consumed, and keyboard focus is visible; finally the sync queue holds nothing but the fixture's own pending job — no marketplace was written to. `docs/ACCEPTANCE-OPERATOR-FLOW.md` (new): the same steps as a manual acceptance script for the packaged exe — preparation with an empty data directory and the synthetic fixture, three DPI turns (100 / 150 / 200 %), a keyboard-only turn with the shortcut list, the step table with expected outcomes, the per-screen common criteria, the out-of-scope list (bundle/variant, inline edit, Kritik Fiyat, XML variant, Fulfillment, Hakediş), the security rule (no live write; only preview and guards are checked where a write scenario passes; synthetic, redacted data) and the result record format. Findings the flow surfaced and their fixes at the source: a dashboard drill that names a record now lands on that record (`DrillThrough` reveals like a workspace link does, #813 rule); the order detail's manual-order form fields (`OrdersPanel.Field`) now tie their caption to the box through `FormField.Labelled`; the channel matrix's status filter is named "Durum filtresi".
