Not done: the automated flow runs the window in-process, not the packaged exe (the written script covers the exe; the CI publishes the same build); the DPI turns scale the content with a layout transform (DIP invariance), not a per-monitor DPI change; the loading state is not held open on purpose in this flow (the busy overlay is #885's and #887's own test), the error state is #887's; the channel matrix's bulk-plan drawer and the import's apply are not clicked (the apply is local, the drawer is #845's test); the report's CSV export is not run (it needs a file dialog and is #848/#880's test); the report and orders detail inner tabs are audited as opened by the flow, not every inner tab; the keyboard turn asserts the cycle, two shortcuts and focus visibility, not a full keyboard-only completion of every step.

## #892 — Source credential health (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-892-source-credential-health off codex/issue-891-operator-acceptance (4eb9cf5)

A source's credential state joins its health without ever showing the credential. `SourceCredentialHealth.cs` (new): `CredentialPresence` None / Saved / Unreadable (presence only; an encrypted blob this machine cannot open is unreadable); the state words UNKNOWN, NOT_NEEDED, VALID, MISSING, INVALID, UNREADABLE (a trailing "?" is "saved but not yet answered"); `PresenceOf(auth)`, `Evaluate(presence, httpStatus, healthState)` — a 401/403 without a saved credential is missing, with one is rejected, a success with one is valid and without one needs none, any other answer keeps the presence and says nothing about the credential, an unreadable blob is a renewal regardless; `AfterRefusal(credentialSent)`, `AfterSave(auth)`, `ShouldFailFast(state)` (missing, rejected, unreadable), `Describe(state)` → `CredentialVerdict(State, Word, Detail, Level)` with the words gerekmiyor / kayıtlı · doğrulandı / kayıtlı · henüz doğrulanmadı / eksik / reddedildi · yenileme gerekli / okunamadı · yeniden kaydedin; `XmlSourceAuthException(httpStatus, credentialSent)` — the download's typed refusal whose message says "enter one" or "renew it" and never a value. `XmlSourceReader.ReadAsync` throws it on 401/403 before the generic status message (the real download entry point fails fast with a diagnosis); `XmlAuthStore.Presence(id, directory)` reports presence without surfacing an exception. `XmlSourceHealthChecker.CheckAsync` gains `XmlAuth? auth` and sends the saved credential on the probe, so a working credential reads as healthy and a rejected one is a credential finding instead of a bare 401. `XmlSource.LastCredentialState` (JSON-stored, default UNKNOWN) persists the word. `MainWindow.xaml.cs`: every probe (the scan, the "Erişimi kontrol et" check, the scheduled health pass) carries `ProbeAuth(source)` and records `SourceCredentialHealth.Evaluate(XmlAuthStore.Presence(…), status, state)` next to the health state; the read's download catch records `AfterRefusal` on the typed exception and persists it to the source (`PersistCredentialState`); saving a credential on the form records `AfterSave`; the scheduled read fails fast — no request — when the last answer said the credential is missing, rejected or unreadable, and records a typed refusal it meets. `XmlSourceHealthPanel`: `SourceHealthFacts.CredentialState` (init property; existing constructions unchanged), a "Kimlik bilgisi" line as the last line, and a missing / rejected / unreadable credential makes the verdict Failed; `XmlSourceListGrouping` bands such a source as a problem named "kimlik bilgisi: …" before the reachability word. `XmlSourceHealthPanelTests` pins the line count at 8 with the new last line.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SourceCredentialHealthTests.cs` (new, 3; they name members that did not exist before): the state follows presence and the last answer (every combination, the "?" states, fail-fast set, the words, the typed refusal's message naming the fact and the status but never the credential); against a Basic-auth feed server the reader refuses without a credential ("enter one"), refuses a wrong one ("renew it") without quoting it, reads with the right one, and the probe reads AUTH_ERROR bare and HEALTHY with the saved credential; a credential saved for one source is invisible to another, never on disk in the clear, and a garbage blob is Unreadable while the other source stays None. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SourceCredentialHealthUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real window with an injected feed server — the access check of the protected feed without a credential records MISSING with nothing sent and the panel says "Kimlik bilgisi … eksik"; the real read refuses with the typed reason and keeps the state; a wrong saved credential is sent, rejected and recorded INVALID ("yenileme gerekli"); the right one is HEALTHY and VALID ("doğrulandı"); the open feed records NOT_NEEDED and never receives the other source's credential; no value is on screen, in any database or in the audit trail; after a restart the last state shows with the source before any probe and at no request.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=SourceCredentialHealth.cs (new), XmlSourceReader.cs, Catalog/XmlSourceHealthChecker.cs, Catalog/Models.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, XmlSourceHealthPanel.cs, XmlSourceListGrouping.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceHealthPanelTests.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SourceCredentialHealthTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SourceCredentialHealthUiTests.cs (new); TEST_RESULT=683 passed, 0 failed (full Release suite; last green run 679, +4); artifact: self-contained win-x64 Release publish, SHA-256 08aabd309059cde5c4824a35cc3cedb7dde81847c7e3fb4b21ec74a2cd680fce (publish/ not committed).

Not done: "expired" is not told apart from "rejected" — an HTTP 401/403 is the only signal a Basic-auth feed gives, so both read as "reddedildi · yenileme gerekli"; an unreadable blob is detected when a probe or read loads it, not scanned proactively; the scheduled fail-fast trusts the last recorded word — a credential fixed outside the app (the server side) is retried only after the operator re-saves it or runs the access check; the support package and the diagnostics page do not yet list credential states per source; the health panel offers no "Kimlik bilgisini yenile" action of its own (the form's fields are the place).

## #893 — Immutable source revision snapshot (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-893-source-revisions off codex/issue-892-source-credential-health (f187467)

Every change to a source's configuration is one immutable, numbered snapshot, written with the source. `SourceRevisions.cs` (new): `SourceConfig` — the part of an `XmlSource` that is configuration (address, schedule, auto-import, mapping with sorted keys, decimal and number culture, price mode / formula / currencies / FX / markup / fixed amount / minimum price, stock rules, brand and category filters, update flags, missing-source grace) as opposed to the state the runs write — with `Of(source)`, `ApplyTo(source)` (identity and state untouched), canonical `ToJson`/`FromJson` and a SHA-256 `Fingerprint` that ignores field order and state; `SourceRevisionSnapshot(SourceId, Revision, Fingerprint, CreatedUtc, Reason, Json)` with `Config`; `SourceRevisionConflictException(loaded, current)` whose message says nothing was overwritten; `SourceRevisionStore` — the `SourceRevisions` table in the catalogue database (primary key source + revision, append-only), `EnsureTable`, `Latest`, `Append(connection, transaction, source, reason)` (the next revision when the fingerprint differs from the latest, else the latest), `List`, `Get`, `SafeReason` (redacted, 80 characters). `XmlSource.ConfigRevision` (JSON-stored) is the revision the record is at. `CatalogStore.SaveSource(source)` now runs through `SaveSource(source, reason)`: inside the existing transaction it refuses a record whose loaded revision is no longer the current one (a never-loaded record, revision 0, carries no expectation — fixtures and upgrades keep working), appends a revision only when the configuration changed, and stamps the source; `SourceRevisions(id)`, `SourceRevision(id, rev)` read the ledger; `RestoreSourceRevision(id, rev)` puts that revision's configuration on the current record and saves it with the reason "geri alma: rev. N" — history is never rewritten. `XmlRunStore` gains a `SourceRevision` column (added in place), `Start(…, sourceRevision)` and `XmlRunRecord.SourceRevision`; the window starts the interactive and the scheduled import runs under `s.ConfigRevision`, and the sync centre's XML history grid shows a "Rev." column, so an import can be read against the configuration that produced it.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SourceRevisionTests.cs` (new, 3; they name members that did not exist before): the fingerprint covers configuration only — mapping order and every state field leave it unchanged, a rule change changes it, `ApplyTo` restores configuration without touching identity or state, the JSON round-trips and rejects garbage, the reason is capped and redacted; configuration changes append revisions (the first save is revision 1, a state-only save appends nothing, a rule and mapping change is revision 2 with its reason and the old snapshot untouched) and the ledger survives a restart with the same current revision and readable history; a concurrent edit (two editors load revision 1, one saves, the other's save) is refused with the loaded and current revisions named and nothing written, a reload saves as revision 3, a rollback to revision 1 is revision 4 with the reason naming 1 and revision 1's fingerprint while revision 3 stays what it was, unknown revisions and sources are refused, and runs record the revision they started under (0 when none).

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=SourceRevisions.cs (new), Catalog/Models.cs, Catalog/CatalogStore.cs, XmlRunStore.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, MainWindow.Sync.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/SourceRevisionTests.cs (new); TEST_RESULT=686 passed, 0 failed (full Release suite; last green run 683, +3); artifact: self-contained win-x64 Release publish, SHA-256 cc491034607111fb1295d35651903b2b235be2dc0fcdcb11fd9e3e0a09141314 (publish/ not committed).

Not done: there is no revision history screen or rollback button yet — the ledger and the rollback are store operations (the sync centre's XML history shows the revision each run used); a source's credential is not part of the snapshot (it lives in the encrypted auth store and is never copied into a revision); the conflict check protects records that were loaded from the store — an object built from scratch with an existing id saves without a check, as every fixture and upgrade path relies on; the ledger has no retention cap of its own (one row per configuration change, capped only by the 64 KB snapshot size); the mapping revision (#8xx `MappingRevision`) and the configuration revision are separate counters — the first is the import's mapping-compatibility guard, the second the operator's history.

## #894 — Feed download retention policy (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-894-feed-cache-retention off codex/issue-893-source-revisions (70ff43f)

Every feed a source downloads is kept on disk under a retention policy, with a last-known-good pointer. `FeedCache.cs` (new): the `feed-cache/<sourceId>/` folder beside the data with a `manifest.json` per source; `FeedCacheEntry(SourceId, FileName, Outcome, FeedHash, Bytes, StoredUtc, RunId, LocationLabel)`; `Store(dataDirectory, sourceId, text, outcome, runId, location)` writes the feed through a temporary file, records it (SHA-256 fingerprint, size, time, run) and applies the per-source keep policy — 5 successful and 2 failed entries, newest kept; `List`; `LastKnownGood` is the newest successful entry that verifies against its fingerprint (a corrupt candidate is dropped and the next considered, never served) and `ReadLastKnownGood` its text; `Sweep(dataDirectory, liveSourceIds, activeSourceIds, quotaBytes)` removes orphans (a directory of a source that no longer exists, a file the manifest does not know, an entry whose file is gone), corrupt entries and manifests, and — under quota pressure — the oldest entries across sources, failed before successful, never a live source's last known good, never anything of a source whose run is in progress, never a temporary file younger than the 10-minute grace; the default quota is 200 MB; `Check(dataDirectory)` is the diagnostics line "Besleme önbelleği" (sources, entries, MB against the quota; WARN over it); `LocationLabel` keeps an address only with its query string and fragment dropped and its user segment masked, so a URL that carried a credential never lands in the manifest; a source id that is not a safe token is refused. Wiring: the import page's read caches the feed as a success right after the download stage, the scheduled read caches it under its run id, both through `CacheFeed` (a cache failure never fails the read); `SweepFeedCache()` runs at the start of every scheduler tick with the live sources and the sources whose runs are in progress and reports what it removed; `DiagnosticsService` adds the cache check; `DataBackupService` leaves the cache folder out of a backup (derived data under its own retention).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FeedCacheTests.cs` (new, 3; they name members that did not exist before): the keep policy trims to 5 successful and 2 failed, the manifest carries the address without its query string and no file outside the manifest, the last known good is the newest success and survives a restart, an unsafe source id is refused, the label drops a fragment and masks a user path; a tampered newest entry is skipped and dropped so the previous good one is served, and the sweep removes a stray file, a deleted source's directory and a missing entry while counting an unreadable manifest as corrupt and reporting OK afterwards; quota pressure evicts failed entries first and then the oldest successes while every live source keeps its last known good and a source with a run in progress keeps everything, over-quota is a warning, a temporary file of a write in flight is left alone and an abandoned one is an orphan. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FeedCacheWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): the real window reads a local feed — it becomes the last known good with a masked address label, the diagnostics line reads one entry, and a data backup carries the catalogue but not the cache.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=3; FILES_CHANGED=FeedCache.cs (new), MainWindow.xaml.cs, AuditStore.cs, DataBackupService.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FeedCacheTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FeedCacheWindowTests.cs (new); TEST_RESULT=690 passed, 0 failed (full Release suite; last green run 686, +4); artifact: self-contained win-x64 Release publish, SHA-256 d5bfc04934822db0bf587a6e1326170bbdf73811cbfc4a4c1636ea9d9d2c385d (publish/ not committed).

Not done: failed downloads have no content to cache (the reader throws before any text exists), so "failed" entries are written only where a feed was read but could not be used — today no caller stores one yet (the API and its retention are in place); the last known good is not yet offered to the operator as a fallback when a download fails (a "use the last good feed" action is the next step); the quota is a constant, not a setting; the sweep runs with the scheduler tick, not on a timer of its own, so a machine that never reaches a tick keeps its cache until it does; the support package does not include cache metadata.
